⸻

1\. Purpose

C1 menetapkan constitutional foundation bagi seluruh Knowledge Architecture PD-04.

C1 menjelaskan bagaimana domain Knowledge & Intelligence diterjemahkan menjadi architecture domain yang memiliki:

• identity;

• ownership;

• architectural boundary;

• knowledge-domain responsibility;

• relationship dengan AIOS architecture;

• relationship dengan Governance;

• relationship dengan Runtime;

• relationship dengan AI Engineering;

• controlled architectural evolution.

C1 menjadi parent constitution bagi:

C2 — Knowledge Model Architecture

C3 — Knowledge Asset Architecture

C4 — Knowledge Repository Architecture

C5 — Knowledge Semantic Architecture

C6 — Knowledge Graph Architecture

C7 — Context Knowledge Architecture

C8 — Knowledge Integrity & Governance Interface

C9 — Knowledge Evolution & Improvement Architecture

C10 — Knowledge Architecture Success

C1 tidak mengambil alih detail yang menjadi concern section tersebut.

4\. Knowledge Architecture Identity

PD-04 adalah Platform Division yang memiliki domain:

Knowledge & Intelligence

Primary responsibility domain tersebut adalah:

• Knowledge;

• Context;

• Intelligence Assets.

PD-04 secara eksplisit memiliki:

• Knowledge Model;

• Knowledge Repository;

• Semantic Layer;

• Context Knowledge.

Cabang Cabang Divisi yang Akan Dibangun.txt

Karena itu Knowledge Architecture merupakan architectural expression dari domain ownership tersebut.

Canonical relationship:

PD-04

Knowledge & Intelligence

│

Knowledge Authority

│

▼

Knowledge Domain

│

Knowledge Architecture

⸻

5\. Constitutional Principle

Knowledge Architecture harus:

1\. tetap berada dalam domain Knowledge & Intelligence;

2\. mempertahankan ownership PD-04;

3\. memiliki boundary yang dapat dibedakan dari Runtime;

4\. memiliki boundary yang dapat dibedakan dari AI Engineering;

5\. memiliki boundary yang dapat dibedakan dari Governance;

6\. memiliki hubungan yang jelas dengan Architecture Office;

7\. menjaga canonical identity Knowledge Model;

8\. menjaga integrity Knowledge Assets;

9\. menjaga semantic consistency;

10\. menjaga Context Knowledge boundary;

11\. mendukung penggunaan knowledge oleh domain consumer tanpa memindahkan ownership;

12\. dapat berkembang secara terkendali.

⸻

6\. Knowledge Architecture Scope

6.1 In Scope

C1 mencakup constitutional definition untuk:

• Knowledge Architecture;

• Knowledge Model;

• Knowledge Assets;

• Knowledge Repository;

• Semantic Layer;

• Context Knowledge;

• Knowledge Graph sebagai knowledge-domain structure;

• knowledge integrity boundary;

• knowledge architecture interfaces;

• knowledge architecture evolution.

Detail masing-masing concern ditetapkan oleh child sections.

⸻

6.2 Out of Scope

C1 tidak memiliki primary ownership terhadap:

• Runtime execution;

• Runtime lifecycle;

• Agent execution;

• Workflow execution;

• Infrastructure implementation;

• Compute;

• Network;

• Cloud platform;

• Security implementation;

• Governance policy ownership;

• enterprise strategic direction;

• organization-wide executive authority;

• AI engineering implementation.

Domain tersebut tetap berada pada Platform Division atau authority yang relevan.

⸻

Hubungan tersebut merupakan architectural relationship, bukan transfer ownership.

15\. Knowledge Repository Boundary

Knowledge Repository merupakan bagian dari PD-04 knowledge responsibility.

Repository architecture harus menyediakan boundary bagi:

• persistence;

• organization;

• retrieval;

• knowledge asset storage;

• knowledge lifecycle relationship.

Namun C1 tidak menentukan:

• database engine;

• storage technology;

• cloud provider;

• infrastructure implementation;

• deployment topology.

Detail tersebut berada pada architecture section yang relevan dan domain implementation owner.

20\. Knowledge Consumer Principle

Platform lain dapat mengonsumsi Knowledge Assets.

Consumer relationship tidak memindahkan ownership.

PD-04

Knowledge Owner

│

│ Interface

▼

Consumer Platform

│

▼

Knowledge Usage

Owner tetap bertanggung jawab terhadap knowledge-domain lifecycle sesuai authority dan governance yang berlaku.

Consumer tidak dapat:

• mengubah canonical knowledge secara tidak sah;

• menetapkan Knowledge Model baru sebagai canonical;

• mengambil alih Knowledge Repository;

• mengubah semantic authority.

23\. Knowledge Architecture Principles

KAP-01 — Knowledge Ownership

Knowledge-domain ownership tetap berada pada PD-04.

KAP-02 — Canonical Knowledge Model

Knowledge Model harus memiliki satu canonical owner.

KAP-03 — Semantic Integrity

Knowledge representation harus mempertahankan semantic consistency.

KAP-04 — Context Integrity

Context Knowledge harus dapat dibedakan dari execution context.

KAP-05 — Repository Integrity

Knowledge Repository harus tetap berada dalam knowledge-domain boundary.

KAP-06 — Explicit Interfaces

Cross-platform knowledge consumption harus melalui interface yang jelas.

KAP-07 — No Domain Takeover

Knowledge Authority tidak boleh digunakan untuk mengambil alih domain Platform lain.

KAP-08 — No Runtime Takeover

Knowledge Architecture tidak menjadi Runtime Architecture.

KAP-09 — No Engineering Takeover

Knowledge Architecture tidak menjadi AI Engineering implementation.

KAP-10 — Controlled Evolution

Knowledge Architecture dapat berkembang, tetapi perubahan material harus tetap traceable dan berada dalam applicable authority.

⸻

27\. Boundary Rules

Rule 01 — One Primary Knowledge Owner

PD-04 merupakan primary organizational owner domain Knowledge & Intelligence.

Rule 02 — Knowledge Authority ≠ Enterprise Authority

Knowledge Authority tidak sama dengan enterprise executive authority.

Rule 03 — Knowledge Authority ≠ Architecture Authority

PD-04 tidak menggantikan Architecture Office.

Rule 04 — Knowledge Authority ≠ Governance Authority

PD-04 tidak menggantikan Governance & Compliance.

Rule 05 — Knowledge ≠ Runtime

Knowledge Architecture tidak mengambil alih Runtime & Execution.

Rule 06 — Knowledge ≠ AI Engineering

Knowledge Architecture tidak mengambil alih Agent, Workflow, atau Capability Engineering.

Rule 07 — Dependency ≠ Subordination

Dependency tidak mengubah ownership.

Rule 08 — Consumer ≠ Owner

Knowledge consumer tidak menjadi knowledge owner hanya karena menggunakan knowledge.

Rule 09 — Boundary Change Must Be Explicit

Material domain-boundary change tidak boleh terjadi melalui kebiasaan operasional.

Rule 10 — Canonicality Must Be Traceable

Status canonical harus memiliki authority dan evidence yang dapat ditelusuri.

30\. Controlled Evolution

Knowledge Architecture harus dapat berkembang tanpa kehilangan:

• ownership;

• semantic integrity;

• traceability;

• compatibility;

• boundary clarity;

• canonicality.

Perubahan tidak boleh mengubah constitutional identity secara informal.

Material constitutional changes harus melalui authority dan review mechanism yang berlaku.

⸻

31\. Success Conditions

C1 dianggap berhasil apabila:

1\. PD-04 memiliki Knowledge Architecture boundary yang jelas;

2\. Knowledge Authority tetap berada pada PD-04;

3\. Knowledge Model memiliki owner yang jelas;

4\. Knowledge Repository memiliki owner yang jelas;

5\. Semantic Layer memiliki owner yang jelas;

6\. Context Knowledge memiliki owner yang jelas;

7\. Knowledge Architecture tidak mengambil alih Runtime;

8\. Knowledge Architecture tidak mengambil alih AI Engineering;

9\. Knowledge Architecture tidak mengambil alih Governance;

10\. Knowledge Architecture tetap aligned dengan Architecture Office;

11\. cross-platform dependency tidak menjadi subordination;

12\. knowledge consumer tidak menjadi knowledge owner;

13\. material knowledge architecture change dapat ditelusuri;

14\. child sections C2–C10 memiliki constitutional parent yang jelas;

15\. tidak ada domain authority yang diciptakan tanpa basis.

⸻

32\. Canonical Constitutional Statement

PD-04 Knowledge & Intelligence holds Knowledge Authority for the knowledge domain of AIOS, including the Knowledge Model, Knowledge Repository, Semantic Layer, Context Knowledge, and associated Knowledge & Intelligence assets within its authorized boundary. PD-04 shall maintain the integrity, ownership, architectural coherence, and controlled evolution of its knowledge domain while preserving the organizational and architectural ownership of other Platform Divisions.

Versi Bahasa Indonesia:

PD-04 Knowledge & Intelligence memegang Knowledge Authority atas domain knowledge AIOS, termasuk Knowledge Model, Knowledge Repository, Semantic Layer, Context Knowledge, serta aset Knowledge & Intelligence terkait dalam boundary kewenangannya. PD-04 wajib menjaga integrity, ownership, architectural coherence, dan controlled evolution domain knowledge tersebut dengan tetap mempertahankan ownership organisasi dan arsitektur Platform Division lainnya.

⸻

36\. Final Freeze Statement

C1 — Knowledge Architecture Constitution is approved and frozen as a source-bounded reconstruction of the established PD-04 Knowledge & Intelligence domain. The section establishes the constitutional boundary for Knowledge Architecture without claiming unsupported historical canonical identity. Any stronger authoritative source discovered by Claude Code shall be treated as evidence for a controlled review, not silently merged into the frozen section.

Final Result: PASS — FROZEN WITH QUALIFICATION

\### Catatan implementasi

Claude Code harus melakukan canonical working artifact, tetapi label \*\*\`BOUNDED RECONSTRUCTION\` wajib dipertahankan\*\*.

Claude Code melakukan source-discovery khusus terhadap literal C1 dan artefak PD-04 di repository/GitHub; bila ditemukan evidence lebih kuat, lakukan review terhadap C1 sebelum mengganti frozen text.

29\. Knowledge Model Change Boundary

Non-material

Perubahan yang tidak mengubah:

• canonical identity;

• semantic meaning;

• consumer contract;

• ownership;

• structural relationship yang material.

Material

Perubahan yang memengaruhi:

• canonical identity;

• semantic meaning;

• core relationships;

• consumer compatibility;

• ownership;

• major knowledge assets;

• cross-platform interface.

Material change memerlukan applicable architecture/governance review.

38\. Knowledge Model Quality Principles

KM-01 — Identity

KM-04 — Semantic Integrity

Apakah meaning tetap consistent?

KM-06 — Compatibility

KM-07 — Canonicality

KM-08 — Boundary

Apakah model tetap berada dalam PD-04 domain?

39\. Failure Conditions

Undefined Ownership

Competing Canonical Models

Untraceable Material Change

Semantic Contradiction

Broken Consumer Contract

Unbounded Domain Expansion

Missing Architectural Context

Unsupported Canonical Claim

Dalam kondisi tersebut:

Do not silently promote the affected model to canonical status.

48\. Success Criteria

C2 is successful when:

1\. Knowledge Model has one clearly identified owner;

2\. Knowledge Authority remains with PD-04;

3\. Knowledge Model is established as the canonical PD-04 reference;

4\. relationship with Knowledge Assets is clear;

5\. relationship with Knowledge Repository is clear;

6\. relationship with Semantic Layer is clear;

7\. relationship with Context Knowledge is clear;

8\. relationship with Knowledge Graph is clear;

9\. relationship with Ontology is clear;

10\. consumer relationship with Runtime is clear;

11\. consumer relationship with AI Engineering is clear;

12\. Architecture Authority remains with PD-02;

13\. Governance Authority remains with PD-03;

14\. Runtime Authority remains with PD-05;

15\. Engineering Authority remains with PD-06;

16\. consumer dependency does not transfer ownership;

17\. material changes have impact-analysis requirements;

18\. canonicality is protected;

19\. semantic integrity is protected;

20\. model provenance and traceability are protected;

21\. unsupported implementation schema is not frozen;

22\. C3–C10 have a clear constitutional dependency on C2;

23\. no competing authority is created;

24\. no cross-platform domain takeover occurs.

⸻

49\. Canonical Knowledge Model Statement

PD-04 Knowledge & Intelligence owns the Knowledge Model as a canonical reference for the Knowledge & Intelligence domain. The Knowledge Model provides the architectural foundation through which knowledge structure and relationships are represented and maintained, and serves as a reference for Knowledge Assets, Knowledge Repository, Semantic Layer, Context Knowledge, Knowledge Graph, and related knowledge-domain artifacts. Its use by Runtime, AI Engineering, or other authorized consumers does not transfer ownership or Knowledge Authority away from PD-04.

⸻

50\. Canonical Boundary Statement

The Knowledge Model is a PD-04 knowledge-domain architecture artifact, not a Runtime, Agent, Infrastructure, Governance, or generic enterprise architecture artifact. Cross-platform consumers may access or depend upon the Knowledge Model through authorized interfaces while preserving PD-04 ownership and authority.

52\. Final Freeze Statement

C2 — Knowledge Model Architecture is approved and frozen as a source-supported core architecture section. The baseline explicitly establishes Knowledge Model ownership under PD-04 and identifies Knowledge Model as the canonical reference of Volume 4. This section therefore freezes the ownership, architectural role, boundaries, relationships, integrity principles, and cross-platform interfaces of the Knowledge Model while intentionally leaving detailed schema and implementation technology unfrozen until stronger authoritative evidence is established.

\## Final Gate

\*\*C2 — Knowledge Model Architecture: \`PASS — APPROVED FOR FREEZE\`\*\*

Qualification-nya jauh lebih ringan daripada C1: \*\*identity dan ownership C2 didukung langsung oleh source\*\*; yang masih terbuka hanya \*\*detail internal model/schema\*\*. Jadi kita tidak perlu menunggu source tambahan untuk construction C2.

• consumer contract;

• ownership;

• structural relationship yang material.

Material

Perubahan yang memengaruhi:

• canonical identity;

• semantic meaning;

• core relationships;

• consumer compatibility;

• ownership;

• major knowledge assets;

• cross-platform interface.

Material change memerlukan applicable architecture/governance review.

38\. Knowledge Model Quality Principles

KM-01 — Identity

Apakah meaning tetap consistent?

KM-06 — Compatibility

KM-07 — Canonicality

KM-08 — Boundary

Apakah model tetap berada dalam PD-04 domain?

Competing Canonical Models

Untraceable Material Change

Semantic Contradiction

Broken Consumer Contract

Unbounded Domain Expansion

Missing Architectural Context

Unsupported Canonical Claim

Dalam kondisi tersebut:

Do not silently promote the affected model to canonical status.

48\. Success Criteria

C2 is successful when:

1\. Knowledge Model has one clearly identified owner;

2\. Knowledge Authority remains with PD-04;

3\. Knowledge Model is established as the canonical PD-04 reference;

4\. relationship with Knowledge Assets is clear;

5\. relationship with Knowledge Repository is clear;

6\. relationship with Semantic Layer is clear;

7\. relationship with Context Knowledge is clear;

8\. relationship with Knowledge Graph is clear;

9\. relationship with Ontology is clear;

10\. consumer relationship with Runtime is clear;

11\. consumer relationship with AI Engineering is clear;

12\. Architecture Authority remains with PD-02;

13\. Governance Authority remains with PD-03;

14\. Runtime Authority remains with PD-05;

15\. Engineering Authority remains with PD-06;

16\. consumer dependency does not transfer ownership;

17\. material changes have impact-analysis requirements;

18\. canonicality is protected;

19\. semantic integrity is protected;

20\. model provenance and traceability are protected;

21\. unsupported implementation schema is not frozen;

22\. C3–C10 have a clear constitutional dependency on C2;

23\. no competing authority is created;

24\. no cross-platform domain takeover occurs.

⸻

49\. Canonical Knowledge Model Statement

PD-04 Knowledge & Intelligence owns the Knowledge Model as a canonical reference for the Knowledge & Intelligence domain. The Knowledge Model provides the architectural foundation through which knowledge structure and relationships are represented and maintained, and serves as a reference for Knowledge Assets, Knowledge Repository, Semantic Layer, Context Knowledge, Knowledge Graph, and related knowledge-domain artifacts. Its use by Runtime, AI Engineering, or other authorized consumers does not transfer ownership or Knowledge Authority away from PD-04.

⸻

50\. Canonical Boundary Statement

The Knowledge Model is a PD-04 knowledge-domain architecture artifact, not a Runtime, Agent, Infrastructure, Governance, or generic enterprise architecture artifact. Cross-platform consumers may access or depend upon the Knowledge Model through authorized interfaces while preserving PD-04 ownership and authority.

52\. Final Freeze Statement

C2 — Knowledge Model Architecture is approved and frozen as a source-supported core architecture section. The baseline explicitly establishes Knowledge Model ownership under PD-04 and identifies Knowledge Model as the canonical reference of Volume 4. This section therefore freezes the ownership, architectural role, boundaries, relationships, integrity principles, and cross-platform interfaces of the Knowledge Model while intentionally leaving detailed schema and implementation technology unfrozen until stronger authoritative evidence is established.

\## Final Gate

\*\*C2 — Knowledge Model Architecture: \`PASS — APPROVED FOR FREEZE\`\*\*

Qualification-nya jauh lebih ringan daripada C1: \*\*identity dan ownership C2 didukung langsung oleh source\*\*; yang masih terbuka hanya \*\*detail internal model/schema\*\*. Jadi kita tidak perlu menunggu source tambahan untuk construction C2.

│

├── Knowledge Representation

├── Semantic Assets

├── Contextual Knowledge

├── Knowledge Collections

├── Knowledge Graph Structures

└── Other Authorized Knowledge Artifacts

Kategori di atas merupakan architectural classification, bukan klaim bahwa baseline menetapkan taxonomy literal tersebut.

14\. Asset Identity

Setiap canonical Knowledge Asset harus memiliki identity yang cukup untuk dibedakan dari asset lain.

Minimum constitutional identity:

• Asset ID;

• Asset Name;

• Owner;

• Authority;

• Status;

• Version;

• Source;

• Relationship;

• Lifecycle State.

Platform baseline mewajibkan metadata platform dan documentation rules yang menekankan ownership, canonical references, versioning, dan artifact integrity.

Divisi yang Akan Dibangun.txt

C3 tidak menetapkan format teknis metadata tersebut.

└── Lifecycle

Supporting owner atau operational custodian dapat ada tanpa menggantikan primary domain ownership.

17\. Asset Integrity

Knowledge Asset harus mempertahankan:

• identity integrity;

• semantic integrity;

• structural integrity;

• provenance integrity;

• ownership integrity;

• lifecycle integrity.

Asset yang tidak dapat dibuktikan ownership atau provenance-nya tidak boleh dipromosikan sebagai canonical asset tanpa review yang sesuai.

• execution-specific representation;

selama tidak mengklaim ownership atau canonicality yang sama.

39\. Asset Quality Principles

Knowledge Assets harus dinilai terhadap:

KA-01 — Identity

Asset dapat diidentifikasi secara unik.

KA-02 — Ownership

Asset memiliki owner yang sah.

KA-03 — Provenance

Asset memiliki source/provenance yang memadai.

KA-04 — Integrity

Asset tidak mengalami unexplained corruption atau contradiction.

KA-05 — Semantic Coherence

Asset konsisten dengan applicable semantic model.

KA-06 — Model Alignment

Asset dapat diposisikan terhadap Knowledge Model.

KA-07 — Lifecycle Integrity

Status lifecycle asset dapat diketahui.

KA-08 — Consumer Safety

Material consumer impact dapat diidentifikasi.

KA-09 — Canonicality

Canonical status dapat dibuktikan.

KA-10 — Traceability

Material change dapat ditelusuri.

Unknown Source

Broken Provenance

Semantic Contradiction

Unclear Canonicality

Broken Model Alignment

Unknown Version

Invalid Lifecycle State

Uncontrolled Consumer Impact

Competing Canonical Asset

61\. Canonical Knowledge Asset Statement

PD-04 Knowledge & Intelligence owns and governs Knowledge Assets within its authorized knowledge-domain boundary. Knowledge Assets are produced and maintained as domain artifacts of PD-04 and remain structurally and semantically related to the Knowledge Model. Their storage, consumption, evaluation, governance, or use by other Platform Divisions does not transfer ownership or Knowledge Authority away from PD-04.

⸻

62\. Canonical Boundary Statement

Knowledge Asset Architecture defines the ownership, integrity, lifecycle, provenance, canonicality, and cross-platform consumption boundary of Knowledge Assets without assuming ownership of Runtime, AI Engineering, Infrastructure, Governance, Security, or Quality domains.

\# 64. Final Freeze Statement

\> \*\*C3 — Knowledge Asset Architecture is approved and frozen as a source-supported core architecture section. The baseline explicitly establishes Knowledge Assets as an output of PD-04 Knowledge & Intelligence and identifies PD-04 as the Knowledge Authority. This section therefore freezes the ownership, architectural role, relationships, integrity principles, lifecycle boundary, canonicality rules, and cross-platform boundaries of Knowledge Assets while intentionally leaving detailed asset taxonomy and implementation schema open until stronger authoritative evidence is established.\*\*

Final Result

C3 — Knowledge Asset Architecture: PASS — FROZEN WITH DETAIL QUALIFICATION

Perbedaan penting dari C1 dan C2:

• C1: bounded reconstruction karena identity constitutional-nya tidak literal.

• C2: source-supported core karena Knowledge Model secara eksplisit canonical reference PD-04.

• C3: source-supported core karena Knowledge Assets secara eksplisit merupakan output PD-04, tetapi taxonomy internal asset belum canonical.

Jadi C3 sudah aman untuk freeze dan construction, tanpa menunggu Claude Code menemukan taxonomy tambahan. Jika nanti Claude Code menemukan source canonical yang lebih kuat, yang direvisi adalah detail taxonomy/lifecycle, bukan ownership dasar PD-04.

Apa posisi Knowledge Assets dalam Knowledge Architecture, bagaimana ownership-nya ditetapkan, bagaimana hubungannya dengan Knowledge Model dan Knowledge Repository, serta bagaimana asset tersebut dapat digunakan oleh Platform lain tanpa memindahkan ownership dari PD-04?

• Knowledge Asset identity;

39\. Asset Quality Principles

Knowledge Assets harus dinilai terhadap:

KA-01 — Identity

Asset dapat diidentifikasi secara unik.

KA-02 — Ownership

Asset memiliki owner yang sah.

KA-03 — Provenance

Asset memiliki source/provenance yang memadai.

KA-04 — Integrity

Asset tidak mengalami unexplained corruption atau contradiction.

KA-05 — Semantic Coherence

Asset konsisten dengan applicable semantic model.

KA-06 — Model Alignment

Asset dapat diposisikan terhadap Knowledge Model.

KA-07 — Lifecycle Integrity

Status lifecycle asset dapat diketahui.

KA-08 — Consumer Safety

Material consumer impact dapat diidentifikasi.

KA-09 — Canonicality

Canonical status dapat dibuktikan.

KA-10 — Traceability

Material change dapat ditelusuri.

Unclear Canonicality

Unknown Version

Invalid Lifecycle State

Uncontrolled Consumer Impact

Competing Canonical Asset

16\. Knowledge Base, Knowledge Graph, dan Ontology tetap berada pada applicable Knowledge & Intelligence boundary;

17\. competing canonical asset dapat dicegah;

18\. asset retirement dapat dikendalikan;

19\. asset evolution dapat ditelusuri;

20\. detailed taxonomy yang belum didukung source tidak dipaksakan;

21\. C4–C10 memiliki dependency yang jelas terhadap C3.

61\. Canonical Knowledge Asset Statement

PD-04 Knowledge & Intelligence owns and governs Knowledge Assets within its authorized knowledge-domain boundary. Knowledge Assets are produced and maintained as domain artifacts of PD-04 and remain structurally and semantically related to the Knowledge Model. Their storage, consumption, evaluation, governance, or use by other Platform Divisions does not transfer ownership or Knowledge Authority away from PD-04.

⸻

62\. Canonical Boundary Statement

Knowledge Asset Architecture defines the ownership, integrity, lifecycle, provenance, canonicality, and cross-platform consumption boundary of Knowledge Assets without assuming ownership of Runtime, AI Engineering, Infrastructure, Governance, Security, or Quality domains.

\# 64. Final Freeze Statement

\> \*\*C3 — Knowledge Asset Architecture is approved and frozen as a source-supported core architecture section. The baseline explicitly establishes Knowledge Assets as an output of PD-04 Knowledge & Intelligence and identifies PD-04 as the Knowledge Authority. This section therefore freezes the ownership, architectural role, relationships, integrity principles, lifecycle boundary, canonicality rules, and cross-platform boundaries of Knowledge Assets while intentionally leaving detailed asset taxonomy and implementation schema open until stronger authoritative evidence is established.\*\*

Final Result

C3 — Knowledge Asset Architecture: PASS — FROZEN WITH DETAIL QUALIFICATION

Perbedaan penting dari C1 dan C2:

• C1: bounded reconstruction karena identity constitutional-nya tidak literal.

• C2: source-supported core karena Knowledge Model secara eksplisit canonical reference PD-04.

• C3: source-supported core karena Knowledge Assets secara eksplisit merupakan output PD-04, tetapi taxonomy internal asset belum canonical.

Jadi C3 sudah aman untuk freeze dan construction, tanpa menunggu Claude Code menemukan taxonomy tambahan. Jika nanti Claude Code menemukan source canonical yang lebih kuat, yang direvisi adalah detail taxonomy/lifecycle, bukan ownership dasar PD-04.

sebagai repository boundary yang mendukung persistence dan access terhadap Knowledge Assets.

Relationship:

C2

Knowledge Model

↓

C3

Knowledge Assets

↓

C4

Knowledge Repository

Conceptually:

Knowledge Model

│

▼

Knowledge Asset

│

▼

Knowledge Repository

│

├── Store

├── Maintain

└── Retrieve

Repository tidak menjadi owner baru bagi Knowledge Assets.

⸻

4\. Repository Identity

Knowledge Repository adalah:

canonical repository boundary untuk knowledge-domain assets yang berada dalam ownership PD-04.

Repository merupakan bagian dari Knowledge Architecture.

Repository bukan sekadar:

• database;

• filesystem;

• object storage;

• vector database;

• graph database;

• external SaaS.

Implementasi teknologi dapat berubah tanpa otomatis mengubah architectural identity repository.

⸻

5\. Repository Ownership

Primary ownership:

PD-04

Knowledge Authority

↓

Knowledge Repository

Knowledge Repository tidak dimiliki oleh:

• PD-05 Runtime;

• PD-06 AI Engineering;

• PD-07 Infrastructure;

• PD-10 Developer Experience.

Platform tersebut dapat menjadi consumer atau supporting domain.

Consumer relationship tidak memindahkan ownership.

Prinsip:

Repository Consumer

≠

Repository Owner

⸻

6\. Repository vs Knowledge Asset

Pemisahan wajib:

Knowledge Asset

\=

Domain Artifact

Knowledge Repository

\=

Repository / Persistence Boundary

Dengan demikian:

Asset

≠

Repository

Asset dapat dipindahkan, versioned, transformed, deprecated, atau archived tanpa mengubah identity PD-04 sebagai domain owner.

⸻

7\. Repository vs Knowledge Model

Knowledge Model menetapkan structural/semantic reference.

Repository menyediakan tempat architectural persistence dan retrieval.

Knowledge Model

│

▼

Knowledge Structure

│

▼

Knowledge Asset

│

▼

Knowledge Repository

Repository tidak boleh menjadi sumber alternatif yang secara diam-diam mendefinisikan Knowledge Model.

Jika repository content bertentangan dengan canonical Knowledge Model, conflict harus masuk ke applicable Knowledge Architecture review.

⸻

8\. Repository Responsibilities

C4 menetapkan repository responsibility pada level architectural concern:

RR-01 — Persistence

Menyediakan mekanisme untuk mempertahankan Knowledge Assets.

RR-02 — Retrieval

Menyediakan mekanisme untuk mengambil Knowledge Assets yang legitimate untuk consumer.

RR-03 — Organization

Memungkinkan Knowledge Assets diorganisasi berdasarkan applicable knowledge structure.

RR-04 — Version Integrity

Mempertahankan identity/version relationship asset.

RR-05 — Provenance Preservation

Mempertahankan provenance yang diperlukan asset.

RR-06 — Lifecycle Support

Mendukung lifecycle state Knowledge Assets.

RR-07 — Access Boundary

Menyediakan boundary akses terhadap authorized consumers.

RR-08 — Integrity

Menjaga repository tidak menjadi sumber uncontrolled knowledge corruption.

⸻

9\. Persistence Boundary

Repository memiliki persistence responsibility.

Namun persistence implementation tidak ditetapkan oleh C4.

Conceptual:

Knowledge Asset

↓

Persistence Boundary

↓

Knowledge Repository

C4 tidak mengunci apakah persistence menggunakan:

• relational storage;

• document storage;

• graph storage;

• object storage;

• vector storage;

• combination;

• atau teknologi lain.

Pemilihan tersebut memerlukan evidence/architecture decision tersendiri.

⸻

10\. Retrieval Boundary

Knowledge Repository harus mendukung retrieval yang sesuai dengan domain knowledge.

Conceptual:

Consumer

│

▼

Knowledge Access

│

▼

Repository Retrieval

│

▼

Knowledge Asset

Retrieval tidak berarti consumer memperoleh direct authority terhadap repository internals.

⸻

11\. Direct Storage Access Prohibition

Consumer tidak boleh dianggap memiliki hak untuk memodifikasi repository secara langsung hanya karena membutuhkan Knowledge Assets.

Preferred boundary:

Consumer

↓

Authorized Knowledge Interface

↓

Knowledge Repository

bukan:

Consumer

↓

Direct Repository Mutation

Kecuali direct access tersebut secara eksplisit diotorisasi oleh architecture dan governance yang berlaku.

⸻

12\. Repository Access Principle

Access harus dibedakan dari ownership.

Access

≠

Ownership

Read

≠

Write

Consume

≠

Govern

PD-04 mempertahankan Knowledge Authority.

Consumer hanya memperoleh access sesuai applicable contract.

⸻

13\. Repository Write Boundary

Write operation terhadap canonical Knowledge Repository harus berada dalam controlled authority.

Write harus mempertimbangkan:

• ownership;

• provenance;

• version;

• lifecycle;

• semantic integrity;

• consumer impact.

Tidak setiap consumer boleh melakukan canonical write.

⸻

14\. Repository Read Boundary

Read access dapat diberikan kepada authorized consumers.

Known cross-platform consumers mencakup:

PD-05 Runtime

PD-06 AI Engineering

Baseline Change Impact Matrix secara eksplisit menunjukkan bahwa perubahan PD-04 memiliki mandatory review relationship dengan PD-05 dan PD-06.

PD-04

Knowledge Repository

│

├──────────► PD-05

│

└──────────► PD-06

PD-05

≠

PD-04

Governance Requirement

≠

Architecture Authority

21\. Repository and Quality

PD-09 memiliki Quality & Evaluation Authority.

Repository dapat menjadi subject evaluation untuk:

22\. Repository Content Integrity

Repository harus menjaga integritas content.

Minimum concerns:

Identity

Version

Provenance

Relationship

Lifecycle

Authority

Repository tidak boleh menjadi dumping ground bagi knowledge artifact yang tidak memiliki identity atau ownership.

⸻

23\. Repository Provenance

Setiap canonical Knowledge Asset yang disimpan harus mempertahankan provenance yang memadai.

Conceptual:

Source

↓

Evidence

↓

Knowledge Processing

↓

Knowledge Asset

↓

Repository

Repository harus mempertahankan kemampuan untuk menghubungkan asset dengan provenance yang applicable.

⸻

24\. Repository Version Integrity

Repository harus dapat membedakan:

Asset v1

Asset v2

Asset v3

apabila versioning material terhadap consumer atau semantic interpretation.

Repository tidak boleh silently overwrite canonical state tanpa traceability.

⸻

25\. Repository Lifecycle

Repository sendiri memiliki lifecycle.

Conceptual:

Proposed

↓

Designed

↓

Validated

↓

Authorized

↓

Operational

↓

Evolved

↓

Deprecated

↓

Retired

Lifecycle vocabulary tersebut merupakan bounded reconstruction, bukan klaim bahwa baseline telah menetapkan state machine literal ini.

⸻

26\. Knowledge Asset Lifecycle vs Repository Lifecycle

Keduanya harus dipisahkan.

Knowledge Asset Lifecycle

≠

Repository Lifecycle

Repository dapat tetap operational sementara individual assets:

• created;

• updated;

• deprecated;

• archived.

Sebaliknya repository dapat mengalami migration tanpa seluruh assets berubah semantic identity.

⸻

27\. Repository Migration

Migration harus menjaga:

• asset identity;

• provenance;

• version;

• relationship;

• canonical status;

• consumer compatibility.

Repository A

↓

Migration

↓

Validation

↓

Repository B

Migration tidak boleh menghasilkan silent knowledge loss.

28\. Repository Replication

Jika replication diperlukan, replicated repository harus dibedakan dari canonical source.

├── Replica

├── Cache

└── Consumer Projection

Replica tidak otomatis menjadi competing canonical source.

29\. Repository Cache Boundary

Cache

≠

• search engine.

Karena itu C4 hanya membekukan:

Repository must support applicable retrieval organization/indexing needs.

Teknologi dan topology tetap open.

Repository

└── Context Retrieval

Semantic Layer

≠

Repository

36\. Canonical Repository Principle

Canonical Repository harus:

0\. memiliki owner;

0\. memiliki authority;

0\. memiliki identity;

0\. memiliki lifecycle;

0\. memiliki relationship terhadap Knowledge Model;

0\. memiliki relationship terhadap Knowledge Assets;

0\. memiliki provenance boundary;

0\. memiliki access boundary;

0\. memiliki version/change traceability;

0\. dapat ditelusuri.

⸻

37\. Single Source of Truth

Untuk Knowledge Repository:

Satu canonical repository boundary harus dapat diidentifikasi untuk setiap canonical Knowledge Asset domain.

Jika terdapat multiple physical stores:

Canonical Logical Repository

│

├── Store A

├── Store B

└── Store C

physical fragmentation tidak otomatis berarti multiple canonical ownership.

⸻

38\. Repository Federation

Federation dapat digunakan jika Knowledge Assets berada pada beberapa repository substrate.

Namun federation harus mempertahankan:

• ownership;

• canonicality;

• provenance;

• identity;

• retrieval semantics;

• source-of-truth designation.

Federation tidak boleh menghasilkan ambiguity:

"Which repository is authoritative?"

⸻

39\. Repository Boundary Rule

Canonical rule:

Knowledge Authority

↓

Knowledge Repository

↓

Knowledge Assets

dan:

Other Platform

↓

Authorized Consumption

Bukan:

Other Platform

↓

Repository Ownership

⸻

40\. Repository Mutation Rule

Material mutation terhadap canonical repository harus dapat ditelusuri kepada:

• initiating request;

• responsible actor/system;

• authority;

• affected asset;

• previous state;

• resulting state.

Repository mutation harus mendukung auditability yang applicable.

⸻

41\. Repository Failure Conditions

Repository harus masuk review apabila terdapat:

Unknown Canonical Source

Broken Provenance

Uncontrolled Mutation

Silent Version Overwrite

Unclear Ownership

Semantic Corruption

Loss of Traceability

Uncontrolled Replication

Unclear Consumer Boundary

Uncontrolled Migration

Jika kondisi tersebut ditemukan, repository tidak boleh dianggap healthy hanya karena masih technically accessible.

⸻

42\. Repository Availability

Availability merupakan operational requirement yang dapat berlaku terhadap repository.

Namun target availability spesifik belum ditetapkan pada source yang tersedia.

C4 karena itu tidak menetapkan:

• SLA;

• uptime percentage;

• RTO;

• RPO;

• replication factor.

Parameter tersebut harus berasal dari applicable architecture, governance, infrastructure, atau operational decisions.

⸻

43\. Repository Security Boundary

Repository harus menerima security requirements dari applicable Security architecture.

C4 tidak mendefinisikan:

• encryption technology;

• authentication protocol;

• authorization engine;

• key management;

• secret management.

Detail tersebut berada pada Security/Infrastructure architecture yang applicable.

⸻

44\. Repository Compliance Boundary

Repository harus dapat memenuhi applicable governance and compliance requirements.

Contoh concern:

• retention;

• access control;

• auditability;

• evidence preservation;

• lifecycle;

• regulatory requirements.

C4 tidak menetapkan regulatory obligations yang tidak disebut source.

⸻

45\. Repository Quality Boundary

Repository quality dapat dinilai berdasarkan:

RQ-01 — Integrity

Content tetap utuh.

RQ-02 — Traceability

Asset dapat ditelusuri.

RQ-03 — Consistency

Representation tidak menghasilkan contradiction yang tidak terkontrol.

RQ-04 — Availability

Repository tersedia sesuai applicable requirement.

RQ-05 — Retrieval Fitness

Asset dapat diakses melalui mechanism yang sesuai.

RQ-06 — Version Integrity

Perubahan dapat dibedakan.

RQ-07 — Ownership Integrity

Owner tetap jelas.

⸻

46\. Repository Evolution

Repository dapat berevolusi melalui:

Capacity Evolution

Retrieval Evolution

Representation Evolution

Storage Evolution

Interface Evolution

Governance Evolution

Evolution tidak boleh mengubah ownership secara implicit.

54\. Repository Governance Rules

C4 menerapkan:

0\. 0. One Canonical Source — canonical source harus dapat diidentifikasi.

0\. 0. Explicit Ownership — owner harus jelas.

0\. 0. Controlled Mutation — canonical content tidak dimutasi tanpa authority.

0\. 0. Traceability by Design — material change dapat ditelusuri.

0\. 0. Consumer Boundary — consumer tidak mengambil ownership.

0\. 0. No Silent Duplication — competing canonical copy tidak dibuat tanpa decision.

0\. 0. Migration Integrity — migration tidak boleh menghilangkan provenance.

0\. 0. Lifecycle Integrity — repository dan asset lifecycle harus dapat dibedakan.

60\. Canonical Repository Statement

PD-04 Knowledge & Intelligence owns the Knowledge Repository as the authorized repository boundary for Knowledge Assets within the Knowledge domain. The repository provides persistence, organization, retrieval, lifecycle, provenance, integrity, and controlled access capabilities for knowledge-domain assets while preserving the authority of the Knowledge Model and the ownership of Knowledge Assets.

⸻

61\. Canonical Boundary Statement

Knowledge Repository Architecture defines the persistence and access boundary for PD-04 Knowledge Assets without transferring Knowledge Authority to Runtime, AI Engineering, Infrastructure, Governance, Security, Quality, or other consumer domains.

\> \*\*C4 — Knowledge Repository Architecture is approved and frozen as a source-supported core architecture section. Physical storage, indexing, retrieval technology, replication, caching, SLA, and detailed interface specifications remain intentionally open until authoritative evidence is found.\*\*

⸻

1\. Purpose

C4 menetapkan architectural foundation dan ownership boundary untuk Knowledge Repository sebagai bagian dari Knowledge Architecture PD-04.

C4 menjawab:

Di mana dan melalui architectural boundary apa Knowledge Assets dikelola, dipertahankan, diambil kembali, dan disediakan kepada consumer yang berwenang?

C4 menetapkan:

• repository identity;

• repository ownership;

• relationship terhadap Knowledge Assets;

• relationship terhadap Knowledge Model;

• repository responsibility;

• persistence boundary;

• retrieval boundary;

• integrity boundary;

• version boundary;

• consumer boundary;

• cross-platform boundary;

• repository lifecycle;

• repository evolution principles.

C4 tidak menetapkan teknologi implementasi tertentu apabila belum didukung source.

⸻

2\. Source Foundation

Baseline Platform Organization menetapkan:

PD-04

Knowledge & Intelligence

Owns:

Knowledge Model

Knowledge Repository

Semantic Layer

Context Knowledge

PD-04 memiliki primary responsibility:

Knowledge, Context, Intelligence Assets.

PD-04

├── Primary Volume

3\. Relationship to C3

C3 menetapkan:

Knowledge Assets

sebagai domain artifacts yang dimiliki PD-04.

C4 menetapkan:

Knowledge Repository

sebagai repository boundary yang mendukung persistence dan access terhadap Knowledge Assets.

Relationship:

C2

Knowledge Model

↓

C3

Knowledge Assets

↓

C4

Knowledge Repository

Knowledge Model

│

▼

Knowledge Asset

│

▼

Repository tidak menjadi owner baru bagi Knowledge Assets.

4\. Repository Identity

Knowledge Repository adalah:

canonical repository boundary untuk knowledge-domain assets yang berada dalam ownership PD-04.

Repository merupakan bagian dari Knowledge Architecture.

Repository bukan sekadar:

• database;

• filesystem;

• object storage;

• vector database;

• graph database;

• external SaaS.

Implementasi teknologi dapat berubah tanpa otomatis mengubah architectural identity repository.

⸻

5\. Repository Ownership

Primary ownership:

PD-04

Knowledge Authority

↓

Knowledge Repository

Knowledge Repository tidak dimiliki oleh:

• PD-05 Runtime;

• PD-06 AI Engineering;

• PD-07 Infrastructure;

• PD-10 Developer Experience.

Platform tersebut dapat menjadi consumer atau supporting domain.

Consumer relationship tidak memindahkan ownership.

Prinsip:

Repository Consumer

≠

Repository Owner

⸻

6\. Repository vs Knowledge Asset

Pemisahan wajib:

Knowledge Asset

\=

Domain Artifact

Knowledge Repository

\=

Repository / Persistence Boundary

Dengan demikian:

Asset

≠

Repository

Asset dapat dipindahkan, versioned, transformed, deprecated, atau archived tanpa mengubah identity PD-04 sebagai domain owner.

⸻

7\. Repository vs Knowledge Model

Knowledge Model menetapkan structural/semantic reference.

Repository menyediakan tempat architectural persistence dan retrieval.

Knowledge Model

│

▼

Knowledge Structure

│

▼

Knowledge Asset

│

▼

Knowledge Repository

Repository tidak boleh menjadi sumber alternatif yang secara diam-diam mendefinisikan Knowledge Model.

Jika repository content bertentangan dengan canonical Knowledge Model, conflict harus masuk ke applicable Knowledge Architecture review.

⸻

8\. Repository Responsibilities

C4 menetapkan repository responsibility pada level architectural concern:

RR-01 — Persistence

Menyediakan mekanisme untuk mempertahankan Knowledge Assets.

RR-02 — Retrieval

Menyediakan mekanisme untuk mengambil Knowledge Assets yang legitimate untuk consumer.

RR-03 — Organization

Memungkinkan Knowledge Assets diorganisasi berdasarkan applicable knowledge structure.

RR-04 — Version Integrity

Mempertahankan identity/version relationship asset.

RR-05 — Provenance Preservation

Mempertahankan provenance yang diperlukan asset.

RR-06 — Lifecycle Support

Mendukung lifecycle state Knowledge Assets.

RR-07 — Access Boundary

Menyediakan boundary akses terhadap authorized consumers.

RR-08 — Integrity

Menjaga repository tidak menjadi sumber uncontrolled knowledge corruption.

⸻

9\. Persistence Boundary

Repository memiliki persistence responsibility.

Namun persistence implementation tidak ditetapkan oleh C4.

Conceptual:

Knowledge Asset

↓

Persistence Boundary

↓

Knowledge Repository

C4 tidak mengunci apakah persistence menggunakan:

• relational storage;

• document storage;

• graph storage;

• object storage;

• vector storage;

• combination;

• atau teknologi lain.

Pemilihan tersebut memerlukan evidence/architecture decision tersendiri.

⸻

10\. Retrieval Boundary

Knowledge Repository harus mendukung retrieval yang sesuai dengan domain knowledge.

Conceptual:

Consumer

│

▼

Knowledge Access

│

▼

Repository Retrieval

│

▼

Knowledge Asset

Retrieval tidak berarti consumer memperoleh direct authority terhadap repository internals.

⸻

11\. Direct Storage Access Prohibition

Consumer tidak boleh dianggap memiliki hak untuk memodifikasi repository secara langsung hanya karena membutuhkan Knowledge Assets.

Preferred boundary:

Consumer

↓

Authorized Knowledge Interface

↓

Knowledge Repository

bukan:

Consumer

↓

Direct Repository Mutation

Kecuali direct access tersebut secara eksplisit diotorisasi oleh architecture dan governance yang berlaku.

⸻

12\. Repository Access Principle

Access harus dibedakan dari ownership.

Access

≠

Ownership

Read

≠

Write

Consume

≠

Govern

PD-04 mempertahankan Knowledge Authority.

Consumer hanya memperoleh access sesuai applicable contract.

⸻

13\. Repository Write Boundary

Write operation terhadap canonical Knowledge Repository harus berada dalam controlled authority.

Write harus mempertimbangkan:

• ownership;

• provenance;

• version;

• lifecycle;

• semantic integrity;

• consumer impact.

Tidak setiap consumer boleh melakukan canonical write.

⸻

14\. Repository Read Boundary

Read access dapat diberikan kepada authorized consumers.

Known cross-platform consumers mencakup:

PD-05 Runtime

PD-06 AI Engineering

Baseline Change Impact Matrix secara eksplisit menunjukkan bahwa perubahan PD-04 memiliki mandatory review relationship dengan PD-05 dan PD-06.

Relationship:

PD-04

Knowledge Repository

│

├──────────► PD-05

│

└──────────► PD-06

PD-05

≠

PD-04

Knowledge Authority

Governance Requirement

≠

Architecture Authority

≠

PD-08 memiliki Security Authority.

21\. Repository and Quality

PD-09 memiliki Quality & Evaluation Authority.

Repository dapat menjadi subject evaluation untuk:

22\. Repository Content Integrity

Repository harus menjaga integritas content.

Minimum concerns:

Identity

Version

Provenance

Relationship

Lifecycle

Authority

Repository tidak boleh menjadi dumping ground bagi knowledge artifact yang tidak memiliki identity atau ownership.

⸻

23\. Repository Provenance

Setiap canonical Knowledge Asset yang disimpan harus mempertahankan provenance yang memadai.

Conceptual:

Source

↓

Evidence

↓

Knowledge Processing

↓

Knowledge Asset

↓

Repository

Repository harus mempertahankan kemampuan untuk menghubungkan asset dengan provenance yang applicable.

⸻

24\. Repository Version Integrity

Repository harus dapat membedakan:

Asset v1

Asset v2

Asset v3

apabila versioning material terhadap consumer atau semantic interpretation.

Repository tidak boleh silently overwrite canonical state tanpa traceability.

⸻

25\. Repository Lifecycle

Repository sendiri memiliki lifecycle.

Conceptual:

Proposed

↓

Designed

↓

Validated

↓

Authorized

↓

Operational

↓

Evolved

↓

Deprecated

↓

Retired

Lifecycle vocabulary tersebut merupakan bounded reconstruction, bukan klaim bahwa baseline telah menetapkan state machine literal ini.

⸻

26\. Knowledge Asset Lifecycle vs Repository Lifecycle

Keduanya harus dipisahkan.

Knowledge Asset Lifecycle

≠

Repository Lifecycle

Repository dapat tetap operational sementara individual assets:

• created;

• updated;

• deprecated;

• archived.

Sebaliknya repository dapat mengalami migration tanpa seluruh assets berubah semantic identity.

⸻

27\. Repository Migration

Migration harus menjaga:

• asset identity;

• provenance;

• version;

• relationship;

• canonical status;

• consumer compatibility.

Conceptual:

Repository A

↓

Migration

↓

Validation

↓

Repository B

Migration tidak boleh menghasilkan silent knowledge loss.

⸻

28\. Repository Replication

Jika replication diperlukan, replicated repository harus dibedakan dari canonical source.

Canonical Repository

│

├── Replica

├── Cache

└── Consumer Projection

Replica tidak otomatis menjadi competing canonical source.

29\. Repository Cache Boundary

Cache dapat digunakan untuk performance atau availability.

Namun:

Cache

≠

Canonical Repository

Cache invalidation, freshness, dan consistency harus dipertimbangkan apabila cache digunakan.

Detail caching architecture belum dibekukan oleh source.

30\. Repository Indexing

Indexing merupakan architectural capability yang secara natural dapat mendukung retrieval.

• indexing model;

• index type;

• indexing pipeline;

• update frequency;

• search engine.

Karena itu C4 hanya membekukan:

Repository must support applicable retrieval organization/indexing needs.

Teknologi dan topology tetap open.

Knowledge retrieval dapat memerlukan berbagai representation.

Repository

│

├── Structured Retrieval

├── Relationship Retrieval

└── Context Retrieval

C4 tidak mengunci satu retrieval mechanism.

Semantic Layer

≠

36\. Canonical Repository Principle

Canonical Repository harus:

1\. memiliki owner;

2\. memiliki authority;

3\. memiliki identity;

4\. memiliki lifecycle;

5\. memiliki relationship terhadap Knowledge Model;

6\. memiliki relationship terhadap Knowledge Assets;

7\. memiliki provenance boundary;

8\. memiliki access boundary;

9\. memiliki version/change traceability;

10\. dapat ditelusuri.

⸻

37\. Single Source of Truth

Untuk Knowledge Repository:

Satu canonical repository boundary harus dapat diidentifikasi untuk setiap canonical Knowledge Asset domain.

Jika terdapat multiple physical stores:

Canonical Logical Repository

│

├── Store A

├── Store B

└── Store C

physical fragmentation tidak otomatis berarti multiple canonical ownership.

⸻

38\. Repository Federation

Federation dapat digunakan jika Knowledge Assets berada pada beberapa repository substrate.

Namun federation harus mempertahankan:

• ownership;

• canonicality;

• provenance;

• identity;

• retrieval semantics;

• source-of-truth designation.

Federation tidak boleh menghasilkan ambiguity:

"Which repository is authoritative?"

⸻

39\. Repository Boundary Rule

Canonical rule:

Knowledge Authority

↓

Knowledge Repository

↓

Knowledge Assets

dan:

Other Platform

↓

Authorized Consumption

Bukan:

Other Platform

↓

Repository Ownership

⸻

40\. Repository Mutation Rule

Material mutation terhadap canonical repository harus dapat ditelusuri kepada:

• initiating request;

• responsible actor/system;

• authority;

• affected asset;

• previous state;

• resulting state.

Repository mutation harus mendukung auditability yang applicable.

⸻

41\. Repository Failure Conditions

Repository harus masuk review apabila terdapat:

Unknown Canonical Source

Broken Provenance

Uncontrolled Mutation

Silent Version Overwrite

Unclear Ownership

Semantic Corruption

Loss of Traceability

Uncontrolled Replication

Unclear Consumer Boundary

Uncontrolled Migration

Jika kondisi tersebut ditemukan, repository tidak boleh dianggap healthy hanya karena masih technically accessible.

⸻

42\. Repository Availability

Availability merupakan operational requirement yang dapat berlaku terhadap repository.

Namun target availability spesifik belum ditetapkan pada source yang tersedia.

C4 karena itu tidak menetapkan:

• SLA;

• uptime percentage;

• RTO;

• RPO;

• replication factor.

Parameter tersebut harus berasal dari applicable architecture, governance, infrastructure, atau operational decisions.

⸻

43\. Repository Security Boundary

Repository harus menerima security requirements dari applicable Security architecture.

C4 tidak mendefinisikan:

• encryption technology;

• authentication protocol;

• authorization engine;

• key management;

• secret management.

Detail tersebut berada pada Security/Infrastructure architecture yang applicable.

⸻

44\. Repository Compliance Boundary

Repository harus dapat memenuhi applicable governance and compliance requirements.

Contoh concern:

• retention;

• access control;

• auditability;

• evidence preservation;

• lifecycle;

• regulatory requirements.

C4 tidak menetapkan regulatory obligations yang tidak disebut source.

⸻

45\. Repository Quality Boundary

Repository quality dapat dinilai berdasarkan:

RQ-01 — Integrity

Content tetap utuh.

RQ-02 — Traceability

Asset dapat ditelusuri.

RQ-03 — Consistency

Representation tidak menghasilkan contradiction yang tidak terkontrol.

RQ-04 — Availability

Repository tersedia sesuai applicable requirement.

RQ-05 — Retrieval Fitness

Asset dapat diakses melalui mechanism yang sesuai.

RQ-06 — Version Integrity

Perubahan dapat dibedakan.

RQ-07 — Ownership Integrity

Owner tetap jelas.

⸻

46\. Repository Evolution

Repository dapat berevolusi melalui:

Capacity Evolution

Retrieval Evolution

Representation Evolution

Storage Evolution

Interface Evolution

Governance Evolution

Evolution tidak boleh mengubah ownership secara implicit.

PD-05 Runtime

sesuai Change Impact Matrix.

• consumers;

• infrastructure dependencies.

apabila applicable.

Platform lain tidak boleh memiliki dependency terhadap repository internals yang tidak tercatat.

Setiap material repository interface harus memiliki:

Contract implementation dapat berkembang selama architectural boundary tetap terjaga.

52\. Repository and Canonical Knowledge

Canonical Knowledge Asset harus dapat ditelusuri ke repository location atau logical identity yang authoritative.

Multiple derived representations tidak boleh mengaburkan canonical source.

53\. Repository and Derived Assets

Derived representation dapat berada pada:

• consumer cache;

• retrieval projection.

≠

Canonical Knowledge Asset

⸻

54\. Repository Governance Rules

C4 menerapkan:

1\. One Canonical Source — canonical source harus dapat diidentifikasi.

2\. Explicit Ownership — owner harus jelas.

3\. Controlled Mutation — canonical content tidak dimutasi tanpa authority.

4\. Traceability by Design — material change dapat ditelusuri.

5\. Consumer Boundary — consumer tidak mengambil ownership.

6\. No Silent Duplication — competing canonical copy tidak dibuat tanpa decision.

7\. Migration Integrity — migration tidak boleh menghilangkan provenance.

8\. Lifecycle Integrity — repository dan asset lifecycle harus dapat dibedakan.

59\. Success Criteria

C4 dianggap berhasil apabila:

1\. Knowledge Repository memiliki identity yang jelas;

2\. ownership berada pada PD-04;

3\. relationship terhadap Knowledge Assets jelas;

4\. relationship terhadap Knowledge Model jelas;

5\. persistence boundary jelas;

6\. retrieval boundary jelas;

7\. access boundary jelas;

8\. write/mutation boundary jelas;

9\. provenance dapat dipertahankan;

10\. version integrity dapat dipertahankan;

11\. lifecycle repository dapat dikontrol;

12\. lifecycle repository dibedakan dari asset lifecycle;

13\. canonical source dapat diidentifikasi;

14\. replication tidak menghasilkan competing canonical source;

15\. migration tidak menghilangkan traceability;

16\. consumer tidak mengambil ownership;

17\. Runtime tetap PD-05;

18\. AI Engineering tetap PD-06;

19\. Infrastructure tetap PD-07;

20\. Governance tetap PD-03;

21\. Security tetap PD-08;

22\. Quality tetap PD-09;

23\. detailed technology belum diklaim sebagai canonical tanpa source;

24\. C5–C10 memiliki dependency yang jelas terhadap C4.

⸻

60\. Canonical Repository Statement

PD-04 Knowledge & Intelligence owns the Knowledge Repository as the authorized repository boundary for Knowledge Assets within the Knowledge domain. The repository provides persistence, organization, retrieval, lifecycle, provenance, integrity, and controlled access capabilities for knowledge-domain assets while preserving the authority of the Knowledge Model and the ownership of Knowledge Assets.

⸻

61\. Canonical Boundary Statement

Knowledge Repository Architecture defines the persistence and access boundary for PD-04 Knowledge Assets without transferring Knowledge Authority to Runtime, AI Engineering, Infrastructure, Governance, Security, Quality, or other consumer domains.

\# 63. Final Freeze Statement

\> \*\*C4 — Knowledge Repository Architecture is approved and frozen as a source-supported core architecture section. The baseline explicitly establishes Knowledge Repository as an owned concern of PD-04 Knowledge & Intelligence. This freeze therefore establishes repository identity, ownership, persistence boundary, retrieval boundary, lifecycle, provenance, integrity, canonicality, and cross-platform ownership rules. Physical storage, indexing, retrieval technology, replication, caching, SLA, and detailed interface specifications remain intentionally open until authoritative evidence is found.\*\*

⸻

1\. Purpose

C5 menetapkan architectural foundation dan ownership boundary untuk Semantic Layer dalam PD-04 Knowledge & Intelligence.

C5 menjawab:

Bagaimana Knowledge Assets memperoleh, mempertahankan, dan menyediakan semantic meaning yang konsisten dalam AIOS?

C5 menetapkan:

• semantic architecture identity;

• semantic ownership;

• relationship terhadap Knowledge Model;

• relationship terhadap Knowledge Assets;

• relationship terhadap Knowledge Repository;

• semantic representation boundary;

• semantic interpretation boundary;

• semantic relationship boundary;

• semantic consistency principles;

• consumer boundary;

• cross-platform boundary;

• semantic evolution principles.

C5 tidak mengunci teknologi semantic implementation apabila belum didukung source.

⸻

2\. Source Foundation

Baseline Platform Organization secara eksplisit menetapkan bahwa PD-04 Knowledge & Intelligence memiliki ownership atas:

PD-04

Knowledge & Intelligence

Owns:

Knowledge Model

Knowledge Repository

Semantic Layer

Context Knowledge

Primary responsibility PD-04 adalah:

Knowledge, Context, Intelligence Assets.

Cabang Cabang Divisi yang Akan Dibangun.txt

Dengan demikian:

Semantic Layer merupakan owned architectural concern PD-04.

⸻

3\. Semantic Architecture Identity

Knowledge Semantic Architecture adalah:

canonical semantic boundary untuk memberikan struktur, hubungan, interpretation, dan semantic consistency terhadap knowledge-domain information yang berada dalam ownership PD-04.

Semantic Architecture bukan sekadar:

• metadata;

• search index;

• database;

• vector store;

• ontology file;

• knowledge graph;

• embedding store.

Komponen-komponen tersebut dapat menjadi implementation mechanisms atau semantic representations, tetapi tidak otomatis mendefinisikan keseluruhan Semantic Architecture.

⸻

4\. Relationship to C2

C2 — Knowledge Model Architecture merupakan structural reference bagi Knowledge domain.

C5 menggunakan Knowledge Model sebagai architectural foundation.

C2

Knowledge Model

↓

Semantic Structure

↓

C5

Knowledge Semantic Architecture

Knowledge Model menetapkan struktur canonical knowledge.

Semantic Architecture menetapkan bagaimana struktur tersebut digunakan untuk:

• meaning;

• relationship;

• interpretation;

• semantic consistency.

C5 tidak boleh secara diam-diam mengganti Knowledge Model.

⸻

5\. Relationship to C3

C3 menetapkan Knowledge Assets sebagai knowledge-domain artifacts.

C5 memberikan semantic interpretation terhadap assets tersebut.

Knowledge Model

↓

Knowledge Asset

↓

Semantic Interpretation

Dengan demikian:

Knowledge Asset

≠

Semantic Architecture

Asset adalah domain artifact.

Semantic Architecture adalah architectural mechanism/boundary yang memberikan semantic structure dan interpretation.

⸻

6\. Relationship to C4

C4 menetapkan Knowledge Repository sebagai persistence/access boundary.

C5 menetapkan Semantic Architecture sebagai semantic boundary.

C3

Knowledge Assets

│

▼

C4

Knowledge Repository

│

▼

C5

Knowledge Semantic Architecture

Repository menjawab:

Di mana Knowledge Assets dipertahankan dan diakses?

Semantic Architecture menjawab:

Bagaimana meaning dan relationship Knowledge Assets direpresentasikan dan diinterpretasikan?

Repository dan Semantic Layer tidak boleh diperlakukan sebagai concern yang sama.

⸻

7\. Semantic Ownership

Primary ownership:

PD-04

Knowledge & Intelligence

↓

Semantic Layer

↓

Knowledge Semantic Architecture

Semantic ownership tidak berpindah kepada:

• Runtime;

• AI Engineering;

• Infrastructure;

• Governance;

• Security;

• Quality;

• Developer Experience.

Consumer relationship tidak memindahkan ownership.

⸻

8\. Semantic Authority

PD-04 memiliki domain authority atas semantic knowledge architecture.

Namun semantic authority tidak berarti PD-04 memiliki authority atas seluruh system architecture.

Boundary:

PD-04

Knowledge Semantics

│

▼

Knowledge Domain

PD-02

System Architecture

│

▼

AIOS Architecture

PD-02 tetap memiliki Architecture Authority.

PD-04 memiliki Knowledge domain ownership.

⸻

9\. Semantic Architecture Responsibilities

C5 menetapkan responsibility berikut.

SA-01 — Semantic Representation

Menetapkan architectural boundary untuk representasi meaning dalam Knowledge domain.

SA-02 — Semantic Relationships

Menjaga hubungan semantic antar knowledge entities/assets.

SA-03 — Semantic Consistency

Menjaga consistency terhadap canonical Knowledge Model.

SA-04 — Semantic Interpretation

Menyediakan basis untuk interpretation terhadap Knowledge Assets.

SA-05 — Semantic Context

Mendukung interpretation yang membutuhkan contextual relationship.

SA-06 — Semantic Evolution

Mendukung perubahan semantic model secara terkendali.

SA-07 — Semantic Traceability

Menjaga hubungan antara semantic representation dan underlying knowledge asset.

⸻

10\. Semantic Architecture Layers

Conceptual model:

Knowledge Model

│

▼

│

▼

▼

Model ini adalah bounded reconstruction dari ownership dan layered architecture yang tersedia, bukan klaim bahwa baseline menetapkan diagram literal ini.

11\. Semantic Meaning

Semantic Architecture harus mempertahankan distinction antara:

Data

Knowledge

Meaning

Context

Inference

Tidak semua data otomatis menjadi knowledge.

Tidak semua knowledge otomatis memiliki semantic interpretation yang sama.

Tidak semua semantic relationship merupakan inference.

⸻

12\. Semantic Structure

Semantic structure harus dapat merepresentasikan sekurang-kurangnya:

• identity;

• type/classification;

• relationship;

• attribute;

• context;

• provenance;

• lifecycle.

Detail schema belum dibekukan.

⸻

13\. Semantic Identity

Setiap semantic entity yang canonical harus memiliki identity yang dapat dibedakan.

Identity harus memungkinkan:

Entity

↓

Semantic Meaning

↓

Relationship

↓

Knowledge Asset

Semantic identity tidak boleh bergantung pada physical storage identifier semata.

⸻

14\. Semantic Relationships

Semantic Architecture harus dapat merepresentasikan relationship antar knowledge entities.

Conceptual:

Entity A

│

├── related-to

├── depends-on

├── derived-from

├── part-of

└── contextualized-by

│

▼

Entity B

Daftar relationship di atas adalah illustrative bounded reconstruction, bukan canonical relationship vocabulary.

⸻

15\. Relationship Integrity

Semantic relationship harus memiliki meaning yang jelas.

Tidak boleh membuat relationship hanya untuk:

• convenience;

• search optimization;

• storage shortcut;

jika relationship tersebut kemudian dianggap sebagai canonical semantic fact.

Rule:

A technical linkage must not silently become a semantic relationship.

⸻

16\. Semantic Context

Semantic meaning dapat bergantung pada context.

Conceptual:

Knowledge

-

Context

↓

Semantic Interpretation

Context Knowledge merupakan owned concern PD-04.

Cabang Cabang Divisi yang Akan Dibangun.txt

Karena itu Semantic Architecture harus menyediakan boundary yang memungkinkan context diperhitungkan tanpa menggabungkan Context Knowledge dengan Semantic Layer secara tidak sah.

⸻

17\. Semantic Layer vs Context Knowledge

Semantic Layer

\=

meaning / relationship / interpretation

Context Knowledge

\=

contextual information

Relationship:

Context Knowledge

↓

Semantic Interpretation

Namun:

Context Knowledge

≠

Semantic Layer

Keduanya tetap distinct architectural concerns.

⸻

18\. Semantic Layer vs Knowledge Model

Knowledge Model

\=

canonical knowledge structure

Semantic Layer

\=

semantic interpretation and relationship boundary

Semantic Layer tidak boleh menciptakan competing model yang bertentangan dengan Knowledge Model.

Jika terjadi contradiction:

Knowledge Model must remain the canonical structural reference unless an authorized architecture change supersedes it.

⸻

19\. Semantic Layer vs Repository

Repository

\=

persistence/access

Semantic Layer

\=

meaning/relationship/interpretation

Satu repository dapat mendukung semantic representation.

Satu semantic model dapat menggunakan lebih dari satu physical repository.

Physical topology tidak menentukan semantic ownership.

⸻

20\. Semantic Layer vs Knowledge Graph

Knowledge Graph dapat menjadi representation mechanism untuk semantic relationships.

Namun:

Knowledge Graph

≠

Entire Semantic Architecture

Graph technology tidak boleh secara otomatis dianggap sebagai canonical architecture kecuali didukung oleh authoritative source atau Architecture Decision.

⸻

21\. Semantic Layer vs Ontology

Ontology dapat digunakan untuk:

• vocabulary;

• concepts;

• relationships;

• classification.

Namun:

Ontology

≠

Entire Semantic Architecture

Ontology merupakan semantic artifact/mechanism.

Semantic Architecture mencakup architectural boundary yang lebih luas.

⸻

22\. Semantic Layer vs Vector Representation

Embedding/vector representation dapat membantu semantic retrieval.

Namun:

Embedding

≠

Semantic Truth

Vector similarity tidak otomatis menetapkan canonical semantic relationship.

Jika vector representation digunakan, hasilnya harus tetap tunduk kepada canonical Knowledge Architecture.

⸻

23\. Semantic Layer vs Runtime

PD-05 Runtime & Execution menggunakan Knowledge.

Namun:

PD-05

Runtime Authority

≠

PD-04

Semantic Authority

Runtime dapat consume semantic interpretation melalui declared interface.

Runtime tidak boleh mengubah semantic meaning secara implicit.

⸻

24\. Semantic Layer vs AI Engineering

PD-06 AI Engineering menggunakan Knowledge untuk Agent, Skill, Workflow, Capability, Planner, dan Reasoning.

Baseline menetapkan PD-06 sebagai domain AI Engineering dan PD-04 sebagai domain Knowledge & Intelligence.

Cabang Cabang Divisi yang Akan Dibangun.txt

Relationship:

PD-04

Semantic Knowledge

↓

PD-06

AI Engineering

PD-06 consumer relationship tidak memindahkan semantic ownership.

⸻

25\. Semantic Layer vs Infrastructure

PD-07 menyediakan technical foundation termasuk storage dan platform services.

Namun:

Technical Storage

≠

Semantic Ownership

Infrastructure dapat menyediakan substrate.

PD-04 menentukan domain semantics.

⸻

26\. Semantic Governance Boundary

PD-03 memiliki Governance & Compliance authority.

Governance dapat menetapkan:

• policy;

• standards;

• controls;

• compliance requirements.

Namun:

Governance Requirement

≠

Semantic Ownership

PD-04 tetap menjadi semantic domain owner.

⸻

27\. Semantic Architecture Authority Boundary

PD-02 memiliki Architecture Authority.

Material changes terhadap Semantic Architecture yang berdampak pada AIOS architecture harus tunduk kepada applicable Architecture Review.

Model:

PD-04

Knowledge Authority

│

▼

Semantic Domain

PD-02

Architecture Authority

│

▼

System Architecture Integrity

Keduanya harus bekerja tanpa dual ownership.

⸻

28\. Semantic Canonicality

Semantic representation dapat dikategorikan sebagai:

Canonical

Derived

Temporary

Experimental

Hanya representation yang secara explicit authorized sebagai canonical yang boleh digunakan sebagai authoritative semantic source.

⸻

29\. Canonical Semantic Source

Untuk setiap material semantic concept:

Canonical Semantic Definition

↓

Semantic Representation

↓

Derived Representations

Derived representations tidak boleh menggantikan canonical definition tanpa explicit authorization.

⸻

30\. Semantic Provenance

Semantic relationship harus dapat ditelusuri apabila material.

Conceptual:

Source

↓

Knowledge Asset

↓

Semantic Assertion

↓

Semantic Relationship

Provenance membantu membedakan:

• observed fact;

• derived relationship;

• inferred relationship;

• manually curated relationship.

Taxonomy tersebut adalah bounded reconstruction dan bukan canonical baseline vocabulary.

⸻

31\. Semantic Assertion

Semantic assertion harus dapat dibedakan dari raw data.

Contoh konseptual:

Raw Information

↓

Knowledge Asset

↓

Semantic Assertion

Semantic assertion tidak boleh diperlakukan sebagai canonical truth hanya karena tersimpan dalam semantic store.

⸻

32\. Semantic Inference Boundary

Inference dapat menggunakan semantic relationships.

Namun:

Semantic Representation

≠

Inference Engine

C5 menetapkan boundary.

Inference-specific architecture harus berada pada applicable Knowledge/Intelligence section.

⸻

33\. Semantic Consistency

Semantic consistency harus dijaga terhadap:

• Knowledge Model;

• Knowledge Asset identity;

• relationship semantics;

• provenance;

• lifecycle;

• canonicality.

Contradiction material harus dapat diidentifikasi dan masuk ke applicable review process.

⸻

34\. Semantic Conflict

Jika terdapat dua semantic interpretations yang bertentangan:

Semantic A

\\

→ Conflict Review

/

Semantic B

Tidak boleh memilih salah satu secara silent apabila conflict bersifat material.

Conflict resolution harus mengikuti applicable Knowledge Authority dan Architecture/Governance mechanism.

⸻

35\. Semantic Versioning

Material semantic changes harus dapat dibedakan berdasarkan version atau equivalent traceable mechanism.

Contoh:

Semantic Model v1

↓

Semantic Model v2

Consumer impact harus dianalisis apabila semantic meaning berubah.

⸻

36\. Breaking Semantic Change

Perubahan semantic dianggap potentially breaking apabila mengubah:

• entity meaning;

• relationship meaning;

• classification;

• interpretation;

• canonical terminology;

• consumer-visible semantic contract.

Breaking semantic changes harus melalui applicable review.

⸻

37\. Backward Compatibility

Jika consumer bergantung pada semantic representation lama, evolution harus mempertimbangkan:

• compatibility;

• migration;

• versioning;

• deprecation;

• consumer impact.

C5 tidak menetapkan compatibility protocol spesifik.

⸻

38\. Semantic Lifecycle

Semantic artifacts dapat memiliki lifecycle:

Proposed

↓

Reviewed

↓

Canonical

↓

Active

↓

Superseded

↓

Deprecated

↓

Retired

State machine ini merupakan bounded reconstruction.

Terminologi final harus mengikuti canonical governance vocabulary apabila ditemukan.

⸻

39\. Semantic Promotion

Semantic representation tidak boleh menjadi canonical hanya karena:

• dibuat lebih dulu;

• digunakan paling banyak;

• tersimpan dalam repository;

• digunakan oleh Runtime;

• digunakan oleh AI Agent.

Promotion menjadi canonical membutuhkan applicable authority.

⸻

40\. Semantic Deprecation

Jika semantic concept tidak lagi berlaku:

Active

↓

Deprecated

↓

Retired

Historical provenance harus tetap dapat dipertahankan.

Semantic deprecation tidak boleh menghapus historical meaning secara silent.

⸻

41\. Semantic Access Boundary

Authorized consumers dapat memperoleh semantic information melalui declared interface.

Preferred model:

Consumer

↓

Knowledge/Semantic Interface

↓

Semantic Layer

Consumer tidak boleh bergantung pada semantic implementation internals.

⸻

42\. Semantic Mutation Boundary

Consumer tidak memiliki canonical mutation authority hanya karena consumer menggunakan semantic information.

Read

≠

Write

Consume

≠

Govern

Canonical semantic mutation harus berada dalam PD-04 authority boundary.

⸻

43\. Semantic Interface

Semantic interface harus mampu mendukung kebutuhan consumer yang applicable.

Potential concerns:

• semantic query;

• entity lookup;

• relationship lookup;

• contextual interpretation;

• provenance;

• version;

• freshness;

• lifecycle state.

Detail API/protocol belum dibekukan.

⸻

44\. No Hidden Semantic Dependency

Platform lain tidak boleh bergantung pada undocumented semantic implementation.

Dependency harus melalui declared architectural boundary.

Consumer

↓

Declared Semantic Contract

↓

Semantic Layer

Bukan:

Consumer

↓

Internal Semantic Storage

⸻

45\. Semantic Integrity Rules

C5 menetapkan:

SI-01

Canonical semantic meaning harus memiliki owner.

SI-02

Semantic relationship harus memiliki definisi yang jelas.

SI-03

Technical linkage tidak otomatis menjadi semantic relationship.

SI-04

Derived representation tidak otomatis menjadi canonical.

SI-05

Semantic change harus traceable.

SI-06

Semantic conflict material tidak boleh diselesaikan secara silent.

SI-07

Semantic implementation tidak boleh mengambil alih Knowledge Model.

SI-08

Semantic consumers tidak memperoleh ownership.

C5 dianggap berhasil apabila:

0\. Semantic Layer memiliki identity yang jelas;

0\. ownership berada pada PD-04;

0\. relationship terhadap Knowledge Model jelas;

0\. relationship terhadap Knowledge Assets jelas;

0\. relationship terhadap Repository jelas;

0\. semantic representation boundary jelas;

0\. semantic interpretation boundary jelas;

0\. semantic relationship boundary jelas;

0\. semantic context boundary jelas;

0\. semantic authority tidak bercampur dengan Runtime authority;

0\. semantic authority tidak bercampur dengan AI Engineering authority;

0\. semantic authority tidak bercampur dengan Infrastructure authority;

0\. semantic authority tidak menggantikan Architecture Authority;

0\. semantic authority tidak menggantikan Governance Authority;

0\. semantic provenance dapat dipertahankan;

0\. semantic canonicality dapat ditentukan;

0\. semantic change dapat ditelusuri;

0\. semantic conflict dapat direview;

0\. breaking semantic change dapat diidentifikasi;

0\. consumer dependency menggunakan declared boundary;

0\. implementation technology tidak diklaim sebagai canonical tanpa evidence;

0\. C6–C10 memiliki dependency yang jelas terhadap C5.

⸻

56\. Canonical Semantic Statement

PD-04 Knowledge & Intelligence owns the Semantic Layer as the authorized semantic architecture boundary for representing, relating, interpreting, and maintaining the meaning of Knowledge Assets in alignment with the canonical Knowledge Model.

⸻

57\. Canonical Boundary Statement

Knowledge Semantic Architecture defines the semantic meaning and relationship boundary of the Knowledge domain without transferring semantic ownership to Runtime, AI Engineering, Infrastructure, Governance, Security, Quality, Developer Experience, or other consumer domains.

\# 59. Final Freeze Statement

\> \*\*C5 — Knowledge Semantic Architecture is approved and frozen as a source-supported core with bounded reconstruction. The baseline explicitly establishes Semantic Layer as a responsibility of PD-04 Knowledge & Intelligence. This freeze therefore establishes semantic identity, ownership, relationship to Knowledge Model, Knowledge Assets, and Knowledge Repository, semantic interpretation boundary, relationship integrity, provenance, canonicality, evolution, and cross-platform boundaries. Ontology, graph, vector, indexing, inference, storage, and protocol details remain intentionally open until authoritative evidence is found.\*\*

C5 tidak boleh secara diam-diam mengganti Knowledge Model.

9\. Semantic Architecture Responsibilities

C5 menetapkan responsibility berikut.

SA-01 — Semantic Representation

Menetapkan architectural boundary untuk representasi meaning dalam Knowledge domain.

SA-02 — Semantic Relationships

Menjaga hubungan semantic antar knowledge entities/assets.

SA-03 — Semantic Consistency

Menjaga consistency terhadap canonical Knowledge Model.

SA-04 — Semantic Interpretation

Menyediakan basis untuk interpretation terhadap Knowledge Assets.

SA-05 — Semantic Context

Mendukung interpretation yang membutuhkan contextual relationship.

SA-06 — Semantic Evolution

Mendukung perubahan semantic model secara terkendali.

SA-07 — Semantic Traceability

Menjaga hubungan antara semantic representation dan underlying knowledge asset.

11\. Semantic Meaning

Semantic Architecture harus mempertahankan distinction antara:

Data

Knowledge

Meaning

Context

Inference

Tidak semua data otomatis menjadi knowledge.

Tidak semua knowledge otomatis memiliki semantic interpretation yang sama.

Tidak semua semantic relationship merupakan inference.

⸻

12\. Semantic Structure

Semantic structure harus dapat merepresentasikan sekurang-kurangnya:

• identity;

• type/classification;

• relationship;

• attribute;

• context;

• provenance;

• lifecycle.

Detail schema belum dibekukan.

⸻

13\. Semantic Identity

Setiap semantic entity yang canonical harus memiliki identity yang dapat dibedakan.

Identity harus memungkinkan:

Entity

↓

Semantic Meaning

↓

Relationship

↓

Knowledge Asset

Semantic identity tidak boleh bergantung pada physical storage identifier semata.

⸻

14\. Semantic Relationships

Semantic Architecture harus dapat merepresentasikan relationship antar knowledge entities.

Conceptual:

Entity A

│

├── related-to

├── depends-on

├── derived-from

├── part-of

└── contextualized-by

│

▼

Entity B

Daftar relationship di atas adalah illustrative bounded reconstruction, bukan canonical relationship vocabulary.

⸻

15\. Relationship Integrity

Semantic relationship harus memiliki meaning yang jelas.

Tidak boleh membuat relationship hanya untuk:

• convenience;

• search optimization;

• storage shortcut;

jika relationship tersebut kemudian dianggap sebagai canonical semantic fact.

Rule:

A technical linkage must not silently become a semantic relationship.

⸻

16\. Semantic Context

Semantic meaning dapat bergantung pada context.

Conceptual:

Knowledge

-

Context

↓

Semantic Interpretation

Context Knowledge merupakan owned concern PD-04.

Karena itu Semantic Architecture harus menyediakan boundary yang memungkinkan context diperhitungkan tanpa menggabungkan Context Knowledge dengan Semantic Layer secara tidak sah.

\=

meaning / relationship / interpretation

contextual information

↓

Context Knowledge

≠

Repository

\=

persistence/access

meaning/relationship/interpretation

Derived

• provenance;

• lifecycle;

• canonicality.

Semantic A

\\

→ Conflict Review

/

Semantic B

Conflict resolution harus mengikuti applicable Knowledge Authority dan Architecture/Governance mechanism.

Semantic Model v1

↓

Semantic Model v2

Perubahan semantic dianggap potentially breaking apabila mengubah:

• entity meaning;

• relationship meaning;

• classification;

• interpretation;

• canonical terminology;

• consumer-visible semantic contract.

Breaking semantic changes harus melalui applicable review.

37\. Backward Compatibility

Jika consumer bergantung pada semantic representation lama, evolution harus mempertimbangkan:

• compatibility;

• migration;

• versioning;

• deprecation;

• consumer impact.

C5 tidak menetapkan compatibility protocol spesifik.

↓

Reviewed

↓

Canonical

↓

Active

↓

Superseded

↓

↓

Retired

State machine ini merupakan bounded reconstruction.

Terminologi final harus mengikuti canonical governance vocabulary apabila ditemukan.

39\. Semantic Promotion

Semantic representation tidak boleh menjadi canonical hanya karena:

• dibuat lebih dulu;

• digunakan paling banyak;

• tersimpan dalam repository;

• digunakan oleh Runtime;

• digunakan oleh AI Agent.

Promotion menjadi canonical membutuhkan applicable authority.

40\. Semantic Deprecation

Jika semantic concept tidak lagi berlaku:

Active

↓

Deprecated

↓

Retired

Semantic deprecation tidak boleh menghapus historical meaning secara silent.

Consumer

↓

Knowledge/Semantic Interface

↓

Semantic Layer

Consumer tidak boleh bergantung pada semantic implementation internals.

Consumer tidak memiliki canonical mutation authority hanya karena consumer menggunakan semantic information.

Read

≠

Write

Consume

≠

Govern

• version;

• freshness;

• lifecycle state.

Detail API/protocol belum dibekukan.

SI-01

Canonical semantic meaning harus memiliki owner.

Semantic relationship harus memiliki definisi yang jelas.
