Technical linkage tidak otomatis menjadi semantic relationship.

SI-04

Derived representation tidak otomatis menjadi canonical.

SI-05

Semantic change harus traceable.

SI-06

Semantic conflict material tidak boleh diselesaikan secara silent.

SI-07

Semantic implementation tidak boleh mengambil alih Knowledge Model.

SI-08

Semantic consumers tidak memperoleh ownership.

Dimension Requirement

Consistency Tidak bertentangan tanpa controlled resolution

Consumer Stability Breaking change dapat diidentifikasi

56\. Canonical Semantic Statement

PD-04 Knowledge & Intelligence owns the Semantic Layer as the authorized semantic architecture boundary for representing, relating, interpreting, and maintaining the meaning of Knowledge Assets in alignment with the canonical Knowledge Model.

⸻

57\. Canonical Boundary Statement

Knowledge Semantic Architecture defines the semantic meaning and relationship boundary of the Knowledge domain without transferring semantic ownership to Runtime, AI Engineering, Infrastructure, Governance, Security, Quality, Developer Experience, or other consumer domains.

\# 59. Final Freeze Statement

\> \*\*C5 — Knowledge Semantic Architecture is approved and frozen as a source-supported core with bounded reconstruction. The baseline explicitly establishes Semantic Layer as a responsibility of PD-04 Knowledge & Intelligence. This freeze therefore establishes semantic identity, ownership, relationship to Knowledge Model, Knowledge Assets, and Knowledge Repository, semantic interpretation boundary, relationship integrity, provenance, canonicality, evolution, and cross-platform boundaries. Ontology, graph, vector, indexing, inference, storage, and protocol details remain intentionally open until authoritative evidence is found.\*\*

1\. Purpose

C6 menetapkan architectural boundary untuk Knowledge Graph sebagai representasi graph-based dari hubungan knowledge dan entity yang berada dalam scope AIOS.

C6 menjawab:

Bagaimana hubungan antar Knowledge dan entitas AIOS direpresentasikan, ditelusuri, dan digunakan sebagai graph tanpa mengambil alih ownership domain lain?

C6 menetapkan:

• Knowledge Graph identity;

• relationship dengan C5 Semantic Architecture;

• graph ownership boundary;

• node boundary;

• edge boundary;

• graph traversal boundary;

• graph integrity;

• provenance;

• dependency analysis;

• impact analysis;

• consumer boundary;

• cross-platform boundary;

• relationship dengan Graphify;

• implementation boundary.

⸻

2\. Source Foundation

AIOS Governance Baseline secara eksplisit menetapkan:

Graphify — Canonical Knowledge Graph Layer AIOS

Graphify ditempatkan sebagai:

cross-cutting boundary

-

read-only terhadap seluruh boundary lain

dan tidak masuk ke linear dependency chain utama.

AIOS GOVERNANCE BASELINE BUNDLE.md

Dengan demikian:

Knowledge Graph merupakan architectural concern yang nyata dalam AIOS, tetapi implementasinya tidak boleh diasumsikan sebagai subsystem internal PD-04 yang berdiri bebas dari Graphify.

⸻

3\. Knowledge Graph Identity

Knowledge Graph adalah:

graph representation yang memodelkan entity dan relationship AIOS secara terstruktur sehingga hubungan, dependency, traversal, dan impact dapat ditelusuri.

Knowledge Graph bukan sekadar:

• database;

• graph database;

• visualization;

• search index;

• ontology;

• vector store.

Physical technology tidak otomatis menjadi canonical Knowledge Graph Architecture.

⸻

C5 menetapkan semantic boundary.

C6 menyediakan graph representation untuk semantic relationships yang membutuhkan graph traversal.

55\. Canonical Knowledge Graph Statement

The Knowledge Graph is the graph representation layer through which canonical AIOS entities and their authorized relationships can be modeled, traversed, validated, visualized, and analyzed. It includes Knowledge as a first-class graph entity while remaining cross-platform through Graphify and preserving domain ownership of PD-04.

⸻

56\. Canonical Ownership Statement

PD-04 owns Knowledge-domain meaning, assets, and semantic authority. Graphify owns the cross-cutting graph representation and traversal boundary. Graphify does not acquire ownership of the domains represented in the graph.

⸻

57\. Canonical Boundary Statement

Knowledge Graph Architecture represents relationships across AIOS without becoming an alternative authority for Knowledge, Runtime, AI Engineering, Governance, Infrastructure, Security, Quality, or Developer Enablement. Graph evidence supports analysis and validation but does not independently create architecture or governance authority.

⸻

\# 59. Final Freeze Statement

\> \*\*C6 — Knowledge Graph Architecture is approved and frozen as a source-supported core with bounded reconstruction. The source explicitly establishes Graphify as the Canonical Knowledge Graph Layer of AIOS, defines its cross-cutting read-only position, establishes canonical node and edge taxonomies, and defines graph modeling, relationship traversal, dependency validation, impact analysis, Repository Knowledge Graph, and Knowledge Graph workstreams. C6 therefore freezes the Knowledge Graph architectural boundary and its relationship to PD-04 without falsely freezing implementation technology or claiming that PD-04 owns the entire cross-platform Graphify layer.\*\* \[oai_citation:22‡AIOS GOVERNANCE BASELINE BUNDLE.md\](sediment://file_00000000e43c81fb92cf05c0fc0f47be)

C6 menjawab:

Bagaimana hubungan antar Knowledge dan entitas AIOS direpresentasikan, ditelusuri, dan digunakan sebagai graph tanpa mengambil alih ownership domain lain?

cross-cutting boundary

read-only terhadap seluruh boundary lain

dan tidak masuk ke linear dependency chain utama.

Knowledge Graph merupakan architectural concern yang nyata dalam AIOS, tetapi implementasinya tidak boleh diasumsikan sebagai subsystem internal PD-04 yang berdiri bebas dari Graphify.

55\. Canonical Knowledge Graph Statement

The Knowledge Graph is the graph representation layer through which canonical AIOS entities and their authorized relationships can be modeled, traversed, validated, visualized, and analyzed. It includes Knowledge as a first-class graph entity while remaining cross-platform through Graphify and preserving domain ownership of PD-04.

⸻

56\. Canonical Ownership Statement

PD-04 owns Knowledge-domain meaning, assets, and semantic authority. Graphify owns the cross-cutting graph representation and traversal boundary. Graphify does not acquire ownership of the domains represented in the graph.

⸻

57\. Canonical Boundary Statement

Knowledge Graph Architecture represents relationships across AIOS without becoming an alternative authority for Knowledge, Runtime, AI Engineering, Governance, Infrastructure, Security, Quality, or Developer Enablement. Graph evidence supports analysis and validation but does not independently create architecture or governance authority.

⸻

\# 59. Final Freeze Statement

\> \*\*C6 — Knowledge Graph Architecture is approved and frozen as a source-supported core with bounded reconstruction. The source explicitly establishes Graphify as the Canonical Knowledge Graph Layer of AIOS, defines its cross-cutting read-only position, establishes canonical node and edge taxonomies, and defines graph modeling, relationship traversal, dependency validation, impact analysis, Repository Knowledge Graph, and Knowledge Graph workstreams. C6 therefore freezes the Knowledge Graph architectural boundary and its relationship to PD-04 without falsely freezing implementation technology or claiming that PD-04 owns the entire cross-platform Graphify layer.\*\* \[oai_citation:22‡AIOS GOVERNANCE BASELINE BUNDLE.md\](sediment://file_00000000e43c81fb92cf05c0fc0f47be)

BELONGS_TO

↓

Domain

Active

Frozen

Deprecated

Retired

26\. Context Qualification

0\. state diketahui apabila material;

0\. access boundary diketahui;

0\. freshness diketahui apabila material.

74\. Canonical Context Knowledge Statement

Context Knowledge is knowledge within the PD-04 Knowledge & Intelligence domain that has an identifiable and defensible contextual relevance to an entity, task, domain, state, time, relationship, or applicable situation, and can therefore serve as an authorized knowledge input to downstream consumers.

⸻

75\. Canonical Boundary Statement

PD-04 owns Context Knowledge and its knowledge-domain semantics, lifecycle, relevance, provenance, and contextual integrity. PD-05 owns Runtime Context and execution context. PD-06 owns Context Engineering and the construction of agent/workflow-specific context. C7 does not transfer ownership between these domains.

78\. Final Freeze Statement

C7 — Context Knowledge Architecture is approved and frozen as a source-supported core with bounded reconstruction. The available source explicitly places Context Knowledge within PD-04 Knowledge & Intelligence alongside Knowledge, RAG, Memory Knowledge, Documentation, and Semantic Layer, while separately assigning Context to PD-05 Runtime & Execution and Context Engineering to PD-06 AI Engineering. C7 therefore freezes the contextual-knowledge boundary without conflating Knowledge Context, Runtime Context, or Context Engineering.

⸻

Final Status

C7 — Context Knowledge Architecture: PASS — FROZEN WITH BOUNDED RECONSTRUCTION

↓

↓

↓

↓

↓

C10 — Knowledge Architecture Success

Catatan penting untuk Claude Code: source discovery lanjutan sebaiknya dilakukan terutama pada repository/canonical artifacts untuk menemukan apakah ada definisi lebih presisi tentang Context Knowledge, karena source yang tersedia saat ini membuktikan existence + ownership + boundary, tetapi belum membuktikan schema/algorithm/implementation model. Jangan mengubah bagian yang saat ini berstatus bounded reconstruction menjadi source-explicit tanpa evidence baru.

1\. Purpose

C7 menetapkan architectural boundary untuk Context Knowledge dalam PD-04.

C7 menjawab:

Bagaimana Knowledge & Intelligence menyediakan pengetahuan kontekstual yang dapat digunakan oleh AIOS tanpa mengambil alih Runtime Context atau Context Engineering?

C7 menetapkan:

• Context Knowledge identity;

• relationship dengan Knowledge Architecture;

• relationship dengan Semantic Architecture;

• relationship dengan Knowledge Graph;

• context knowledge scope;

• context composition boundary;

• context relevance;

• context provenance;

• context freshness;

• context lifecycle;

• context access;

• relationship dengan Runtime Context;

• relationship dengan Context Engineering;

• cross-platform boundary.

⸻

2\. Source Foundation

Source organisasi AIOS secara eksplisit menetapkan PD-04 sebagai:

PD-04

Knowledge & Intelligence

dengan responsibility:

Knowledge

RAG

Memory Knowledge

Context Knowledge

Documentation

Semantic Layer

Divisi yang Akan Dibangun.txt

Dengan demikian:

Context Knowledge merupakan bagian resmi dari domain Knowledge & Intelligence.

Namun source juga membedakan context pada domain lain.

PD-05 mengelola:

Runtime

Session

Context

Execution

Scheduling

Lifecycle

Divisi yang Akan Dibangun.txt

PD-06 mengelola:

Agent

Workflow

Skill

Capability

Planner

Reasoning

Context Engineering

Divisi yang Akan Dibangun.txt

Context Knowledge

≠

Runtime Context

≠

Context Engineering

3\. Context Knowledge Identity

Context Knowledge adalah:

knowledge yang memiliki contextual relevance sehingga dapat digunakan untuk memahami, membatasi, menginterpretasikan, atau memilih Knowledge yang relevan terhadap suatu situation, task, entity, session, atau execution context.

Context Knowledge bukan sekadar:

• metadata;

• session state;

• prompt;

• memory;

• runtime state;

• user input;

• arbitrary retrieved documents.

Contextual Knowledge

dan bukan:

PD-04

Knowledge Authority

│

▼

Context Knowledge

│

▼

Contextual Relevance

20\. Temporal Context

Temporal context menjawab:

Kapan knowledge tersebut relevan?

Context dapat memiliki:

Effective From

Effective Until

Observed At

Updated At

Exact temporal schema tetap terbuka.

⸻

21\. Domain Context

Knowledge dapat memiliki contextual relationship dengan:

Platform

Domain

Capability

Workflow

Subsystem

Domain context harus menghormati ownership boundary.

⸻

22\. Relationship Context

Knowledge dapat menjadi contextually relevant karena hubungan terhadap entity lain.

Contoh:

Agent

↓

USES

↓

Knowledge

atau:

Knowledge

↓

BELONGS_TO

↓

Domain

Relationship harus menggunakan canonical relationship model apabila tersedia.

⸻

23\. State Context

Context Knowledge dapat bergantung pada state tertentu.

Contoh conceptual:

Draft

Active

Frozen

Deprecated

Retired

Context consumer harus mengetahui state apabila state tersebut material terhadap validity.

⸻

24\. Environmental Context

Environmental context dapat mencakup:

• operating environment;

• deployment environment;

• organizational environment;

• technical environment;

• execution condition.

PD-04 tidak menjadi owner atas environment itself.

⸻

25\. Authority Context

Knowledge relevance dapat bergantung pada authority boundary.

Contoh:

Knowledge

↓

Applicable Authority

↓

Authorized Consumer

Context Knowledge tidak boleh mengabaikan authorization.

⸻

26\. Context Qualification

Sebuah Knowledge Asset dapat menjadi Context Knowledge apabila:

1\. identity diketahui;

2\. contextual relationship diketahui;

3\. relevance dapat dijelaskan;

4\. provenance tersedia apabila material;

5\. state diketahui apabila material;

6\. access boundary diketahui;

7\. freshness diketahui apabila material.

⸻

27\. Context Qualification Failure

Jika contextual relevance tidak dapat dibuktikan:

Knowledge

↓

Context Qualification

↓

Insufficient Evidence

Jangan memaksakan classification:

Contextually Relevant

hanya karena retrieval score tinggi.

⸻

28\. Context Retrieval

Context Knowledge dapat menjadi input bagi retrieval.

Conceptual:

Context

↓

Contextual Qualification

↓

Knowledge Retrieval

↓

Relevant Knowledge

Exact retrieval algorithm belum ditetapkan oleh C7.

⸻

29\. Relationship to RAG

Source menempatkan RAG dalam PD-04 Knowledge & Intelligence.

Divisi yang Akan Dibangun.txt

Dengan demikian C7 dapat berfungsi sebagai contextual layer bagi RAG:

Context

↓

Context Knowledge

↓

RAG Retrieval

↓

Relevant Knowledge

C7 tidak menetapkan satu implementation pattern untuk RAG.

⸻

30\. Context-Aware Retrieval

Context-aware retrieval dapat menggunakan:

• semantic relevance;

• entity relationship;

• task relevance;

• temporal relevance;

• domain relevance;

• graph relationship;

• authorization boundary.

Semua factor harus memiliki rationale.

⸻

31\. Context and Knowledge Graph

C6 dapat menyediakan contextual relationship discovery.

Knowledge Graph

↓

Related Entities

↓

Contextual Qualification

↓

Context Knowledge

Graph traversal adalah evidence/input.

Bukan automatic contextual truth.

⸻

32\. Context and Semantic Layer

C5 semantic architecture membantu:

• meaning;

• concept relationships;

• terminology;

• classification;

• semantic similarity.

C7 menambahkan:

contextual relevance terhadap situation.

⸻

33\. Context Provenance

Context Knowledge harus dapat menjawab:

Where did this contextual relationship come from?

Why is it considered relevant?

When was it established?

Which source supports it?

Material context claims harus traceable.

⸻

34\. Context Provenance Chain

Canonical pattern:

Source

↓

Knowledge Asset

↓

Semantic Interpretation

↓

Contextual Qualification

↓

Context Knowledge

Jika provenance chain terputus:

Context Confidence

↓

Reduced

atau:

Insufficient Evidence

sesuai materiality.

⸻

35\. Context Confidence

Context relevance dapat memiliki confidence.

Contoh:

High

Medium

Low

Insufficient Evidence

Confidence menjelaskan strength of evidence.

Confidence bukan authorization.

⸻

36\. Context Freshness

Context Knowledge harus mempertimbangkan freshness apabila contextual validity berubah terhadap waktu.

Fresh

Aging

Stale

Expired

Unknown

Exact thresholds bersifat domain-specific dan belum dibekukan.

⸻

37\. Stale Context

Context Knowledge yang stale tidak otomatis harus dihapus.

Possible states:

Historical

Inactive

Stale

Superseded

Deprecated

Consumer harus mengetahui statusnya.

⸻

38\. Context Lifecycle

Context Knowledge lifecycle:

Candidate

↓

Qualified

↓

Available

↓

Consumed

↓

Reviewed

↓

Updated / Superseded

↓

Retired

Lifecycle implementation belum ditentukan.

⸻

39\. Context Update

Context Knowledge dapat berubah apabila:

• source berubah;

• relationship berubah;

• semantic interpretation berubah;

• domain state berubah;

• temporal validity berubah;

• authorization boundary berubah.

Material change harus traceable.

⸻

40\. Context Invalidation

Context Knowledge harus dapat dianggap invalid apabila:

Source Invalid

Relationship Invalid

Authority Invalid

Temporal Scope Expired

Semantic Interpretation Superseded

Invalid context tidak boleh diperlakukan sebagai active context tanpa qualification.

⸻

41\. Context Composition

Context Knowledge dapat dikomposisikan dari beberapa knowledge elements.

Conceptual:

Context

├── Entity Knowledge

├── Task Knowledge

├── Temporal Knowledge

├── Domain Knowledge

└── Relationship Knowledge

C7 tidak menetapkan final context-window composition.

⸻

42\. Context Prioritization Boundary

Context prioritization dapat menggunakan relevance.

Namun prioritization untuk:

• token budget;

• prompt construction;

• reasoning strategy;

• agent execution

berada pada consumer / applicable domain.

PD-04 menyediakan contextual knowledge signals.

⸻

PD-04

Contextual Knowledge Selection

↓

PD-06

Context Engineering

↓

PD-05

Runtime Context

↓

↓

≠

Context

Context Knowledge menjelaskan contextual relevance.

45\. Context Knowledge and Documentation

Documentation merupakan bagian dari PD-04.

↓

46\. Context Authority

PD-04

Knowledge Authority

• contextual provenance.

Bukan authority terhadap runtime context execution.

Runtime Context

Session

Execution

Lifecycle

Divisi yang Akan Dibangun.txt

Context Knowledge access harus menghormati:

Authentication

Authorization

Secrets

Protection

50\. Governance Boundary

Governance requirements terhadap:

• retention;

• certification;

• compliance;

• access;

• lifecycle

tetap mengikuti PD-03 authority.

51\. Architecture Boundary

PD-02 tetap memiliki authority terhadap:

• system architecture;

• domain boundary;

• architectural consistency;

• architecture decisions.

C7 merupakan domain architecture PD-04 yang harus tetap aligned dengan canonical architecture.

52\. Context Access

Context Knowledge consumer harus melewati applicable authorization.

↓

Access Request

↓

Authorization

↓

Context Knowledge

⸻

53\. Context Isolation

Context Knowledge dari satu domain tidak boleh otomatis dianggap applicable terhadap domain lain.

Example:

Domain A Context

≠

Domain B Context

Cross-domain reuse membutuhkan contextual validity.

54\. Cross-Platform Context

Context Knowledge dapat digunakan lintas platform.

Contoh:

PD-04

Context Knowledge

↓

PD-06

Context Engineering

↓

PD-05

Runtime Context

Cross-platform use tidak memindahkan ownership.

55\. Context Contract

Consumer-facing Context Knowledge output harus memiliki minimum conceptual information:

Element Requirement

Knowledge Identity Required

Contextual Relevance Required

Provenance Required when material

Freshness Required when material

State Required when material

Confidence Required when applicable

Access Boundary Required

Source Authority Required

Physical API/schema belum dibekukan.

⸻

56\. Context Integrity

Context integrity harus menjaga:

• relevance;

• provenance;

• freshness;

• identity;

• state;

• authorization;

• semantic consistency.

Context yang kehilangan integrity tidak boleh diperlakukan sebagai canonical contextual evidence.

57\. Context Contradiction

Jika dua Context Knowledge elements bertentangan:

Context A

-

Context B

↓

Conflict Detection

↓

Evidence Review

↓

Resolution / Escalation

Tidak boleh memilih salah satu hanya berdasarkan recency tanpa mempertimbangkan authority dan evidence.

⸻

58\. Context Precedence

Jika precedence diperlukan, basisnya harus mempertimbangkan:

1\. authority;

2\. validity;

3\. scope;

4\. temporal applicability;

5\. provenance;

6\. semantic compatibility.

Tidak ada universal precedence rule yang dibekukan oleh C7.

Consumer

Usage

Accuracy

Consistency

64\. No Context Fabrication

Jika Context Knowledge tidak tersedia:

Do not manufacture contextual knowledge.

Status harus menyatakan:

Context Unavailable

atau applicable equivalent.

⸻

65\. Context Completeness

Context completeness harus dinilai terhadap task/domain requirement.

Tidak semua context harus lengkap secara absolute.

↓

Available Context

↓

Context Gap

harus dinyatakan.

66\. Context and RAG Failure

Jika retrieval menghasilkan knowledge tetapi contextual relevance tidak cukup:

Retrieved

≠

Contextually Valid

67\. Context and Graph Failure

Jika graph relationship ditemukan:

Graph Relationship

≠

Contextual Truth

Similar Meaning

tidak otomatis berarti:

Applicable Context

Semantic relevance dan contextual relevance harus dibedakan.

• new context dimensions ditemukan;

• Knowledge ecosystem berubah;

• Runtime interface berubah;

• Context Engineering interface berubah.

73\. Success Criteria

C7 dianggap berhasil apabila:

1\. Context Knowledge identity jelas;

2\. relationship dengan Knowledge Architecture jelas;

3\. relationship dengan Semantic Architecture jelas;

4\. relationship dengan Knowledge Graph jelas;

5\. relationship dengan RAG jelas;

6\. relationship dengan Memory Knowledge jelas;

7\. Runtime Context boundary jelas;

8\. Context Engineering boundary jelas;

9\. contextual relevance dapat dijelaskan;

10\. context provenance dapat ditelusuri;

11\. context freshness dapat dinyatakan;

12\. context state dapat dinyatakan;

13\. authorization boundary dipertahankan;

14\. context contradiction dapat ditangani;

15\. context failure state dapat dinyatakan;

16\. context completeness dapat dinilai;

17\. context tidak difabrikasi ketika evidence tidak cukup;

18\. cross-platform consumption tidak memindahkan ownership;

19\. PD-04 tetap menjadi Knowledge Authority;

20\. PD-05 tetap menjadi Runtime Authority;

21\. PD-06 tetap menjadi Engineering Authority;

22\. PD-08 tetap menjadi Security Authority;

23\. PD-09 tetap menjadi Quality Authority;

24\. C8 dapat dibangun tanpa mengambil alih C7;

25\. implementation technology tetap open sampai authoritative decision ditemukan.

⸻

74\. Canonical Context Knowledge Statement

Context Knowledge is knowledge within the PD-04 Knowledge & Intelligence domain that has an identifiable and defensible contextual relevance to an entity, task, domain, state, time, relationship, or applicable situation, and can therefore serve as an authorized knowledge input to downstream consumers.

⸻

75\. Canonical Boundary Statement

PD-04 owns Context Knowledge and its knowledge-domain semantics, lifecycle, relevance, provenance, and contextual integrity. PD-05 owns Runtime Context and execution context. PD-06 owns Context Engineering and the construction of agent/workflow-specific context. C7 does not transfer ownership between these domains.

⸻

78\. Final Freeze Statement

C7 — Context Knowledge Architecture is approved and frozen as a source-supported core with bounded reconstruction. The available source explicitly places Context Knowledge within PD-04 Knowledge & Intelligence alongside Knowledge, RAG, Memory Knowledge, Documentation, and Semantic Layer, while separately assigning Context to PD-05 Runtime & Execution and Context Engineering to PD-06 AI Engineering. C7 therefore freezes the contextual-knowledge boundary without conflating Knowledge Context, Runtime Context, or Context Engineering.

⸻

Final Status

C7 — Context Knowledge Architecture: PASS — FROZEN WITH BOUNDED RECONSTRUCTION

↓

C6 — Knowledge Graph Architecture

↓

↓

↓

↓

C10 — Knowledge Architecture Success

Catatan penting untuk Claude Code: source discovery lanjutan sebaiknya dilakukan terutama pada repository/canonical artifacts untuk menemukan apakah ada definisi lebih presisi tentang Context Knowledge, karena source yang tersedia saat ini membuktikan existence + ownership + boundary, tetapi belum membuktikan schema/algorithm/implementation model. Jangan mengubah bagian yang saat ini berstatus bounded reconstruction menjadi source-explicit tanpa evidence baru.

\# Volume 4 — PD-04 Knowledge & Intelligence

\# Part C — Knowledge Architecture

\# C8 — Knowledge Integrity & Governance Interface

\> \*\*Section ID:\*\* C8

\> \*\*Section Name:\*\* Knowledge Integrity & Governance Interface

\> \*\*Platform ID:\*\* PD-04

\> \*\*Official Name:\*\* Knowledge & Intelligence

\> \*\*Volume:\*\* Volume 4

\> \*\*Gold Standard Review:\*\* PASS

\> \*\*Freeze Decision:\*\* APPROVED

1\. Purpose

C8 menetapkan bagaimana Knowledge Integrity dalam PD-04 berhubungan dengan Governance & Compliance tanpa memindahkan ownership antara kedua domain.

C8 menjawab:

• apa yang dimaksud dengan integrity terhadap Knowledge;

• bagaimana integrity dipertahankan;

• bagaimana governance requirement berinteraksi dengan Knowledge;

• bagaimana evidence dan provenance dipertahankan;

• bagaimana compliance concern terhadap Knowledge ditangani;

• bagaimana governance assessment menerima evidence dari PD-04;

• bagaimana finding dan corrective action dikembalikan kepada Knowledge owner;

• bagaimana boundary PD-04 dan PD-03 dipertahankan.

⸻

2\. Source Foundation

Platform Organization menetapkan:

PD-03 → Governance & Compliance → Governance Authority

PD-04 → Knowledge & Intelligence → Knowledge Authority

PD-04 memiliki primary output:

Knowledge Assets

dan mengonsumsi:

Architecture

Governance

Divisi yang Akan Dibangun.txt

Document Relationship Matrix juga menempatkan Volume 4 sebagai otoritas terhadap domain Knowledge & Intelligence, dengan Governance sebagai salah satu relationship lintas platform.

Divisi yang Akan Dibangun.txt

⸻

3\. Architectural Interpretation

Source belum memberikan section literal bernama:

C8 — Knowledge Integrity & Governance Interface

Karena itu C8 menggunakan:

bounded reconstruction

atas tiga source-supported concerns:

Knowledge Authority

-

Knowledge Integrity

-

Governance Interface

Reconstruction ini tidak mengklaim bahwa seluruh detail C8 merupakan literal baseline.

⸻

4\. Primary Boundary

Canonical boundary:

PD-04

Knowledge & Intelligence

│

│ owns

▼

Knowledge Integrity

│

│ governance interface

▼

PD-03

Governance & Compliance

PD-04:

owns Knowledge.

PD-03:

owns Governance & Compliance.

Hubungan tersebut tidak mengubah ownership.

⸻

5\. Fundamental Principle

Knowledge Integrity is owned by the Knowledge domain; governance assurance is exercised through the Governance & Compliance interface without transferring Knowledge ownership to the governance domain.

Model:

Knowledge Integrity

↓

PD-04 Ownership

↓

Governance Interface

↓

PD-03 Governance / Compliance

⸻

6\. Knowledge Integrity Definition

Untuk C8, Knowledge Integrity berarti:

kemampuan Knowledge ecosystem untuk mempertahankan identity, correctness, provenance, consistency, lifecycle state, authority, dan traceability yang diperlukan agar Knowledge dapat dipercaya dan digunakan sesuai scope-nya.

Knowledge Integrity bukan sekadar:

• data availability;

• database consistency;

• retrieval accuracy;

• security;

• compliance status.

⸻

7\. Integrity Dimensions

C8 menggunakan integrity dimensions berikut:

Identity

Provenance

Traceability

Consistency

Validity

Freshness

Authority

Lifecycle Integrity

Access Integrity

Context Integrity

Tidak semua dimension wajib memiliki implementation mechanism yang sama.

⸻

8\. Knowledge Identity Integrity

Setiap canonical Knowledge object harus memiliki identity yang dapat dibedakan.

Identity integrity mencegah:

• duplicate identity;

• ambiguous identity;

• accidental replacement;

• undocumented mutation.

⸻

9\. Provenance Integrity

Material Knowledge harus dapat ditelusuri kepada source atau origin yang relevan.

Canonical flow:

Source

↓

Knowledge Asset

↓

Transformation / Interpretation

↓

Knowledge

↓

Consumer

Provenance harus dipertahankan sepanjang lifecycle apabila material.

⸻

10\. Traceability Integrity

Knowledge harus dapat ditelusuri:

Source

↓

Knowledge

↓

Context / Relationship

↓

Consumer

↓

Outcome

Traceability memungkinkan:

• review;

• correction;

• audit;

• investigation;

• governance assessment.

⸻

11\. Consistency Integrity

Knowledge harus menjaga consistency terhadap:

• canonical terminology;

• Knowledge Model;

• semantic model;

• graph relationships;

• applicable state;

• authoritative source.

Consistency tidak berarti seluruh Knowledge harus identik.

⸻

12\. Validity Integrity

Knowledge harus memiliki basis untuk menentukan apakah knowledge tersebut masih valid dalam scope yang berlaku.

Validity dapat dipengaruhi oleh:

• source status;

• temporal validity;

• authority;

• supersession;

• contradiction;

• lifecycle state.

⸻

13\. Freshness Integrity

Knowledge yang berubah terhadap waktu harus dapat menunjukkan freshness apabila freshness material terhadap penggunaannya.

Model:

Fresh

↓

Aging

↓

Stale

↓

Expired / Superseded

Exact threshold tidak dibekukan oleh C8.

⸻

14\. Authority Integrity

Knowledge harus dapat dibedakan berdasarkan authority source.

Model:

Authoritative Source

↓

Knowledge

↓

Authority Metadata

Retrieval popularity tidak boleh menggantikan source authority.

⸻

15\. Lifecycle Integrity

Knowledge lifecycle harus mempertahankan state yang dapat dipahami.

Conceptual lifecycle:

Candidate

↓

Validated

↓

Active

↓

Reviewed

↓

Updated / Superseded

↓

Retired

State tidak boleh berubah secara material tanpa traceability yang memadai.

⸻

16\. Access Integrity

Knowledge access harus menghormati applicable:

• identity;

• authorization;

• access boundary;

• confidentiality;

• security requirement.

C8 tidak mengambil ownership Security dari PD-08.

⸻

17\. Context Integrity

Context Knowledge dari C7 harus mempertahankan:

• contextual relevance;

• provenance;

• freshness;

• state;

• authority;

• applicability.

Context Knowledge yang kehilangan integrity tidak boleh diperlakukan sebagai trusted contextual evidence tanpa qualification.

⸻

18\. Integrity vs Accuracy

Accuracy

≠

Integrity

Knowledge dapat memiliki integrity yang baik karena provenance dan lifecycle-nya lengkap, tetapi ternyata source-nya sendiri salah.

Karena itu:

Integrity

-

Validation / Evaluation

harus dibedakan.

⸻

19\. Relationship to C1

C1 menetapkan constitutional boundary Knowledge Architecture.

C8:

C1

Knowledge Constitution

↓

73\. Canonical Knowledge Integrity Statement

Knowledge Integrity is the preservation of trustworthy identity, provenance, traceability, validity, consistency, authority, lifecycle, context, and applicable access conditions across the Knowledge & Intelligence domain.

⸻

74\. Canonical Governance Interface Statement

PD-04 owns Knowledge Integrity within the Knowledge & Intelligence domain. PD-03 owns Governance & Compliance and provides the applicable governance, compliance, control, certification, and assurance interface. Governance interaction does not transfer Knowledge ownership, implementation ownership, or domain authority from PD-04 to PD-03.

78\. Final Freeze Statement

C8 — Knowledge Integrity & Governance Interface is approved and frozen as a source-supported foundation with bounded reconstruction. The source establishes PD-04 as Knowledge Authority and establishes Governance as a cross-platform dependency/interface, while preserving domain ownership. C8 therefore defines the integrity boundary of Knowledge and the interface through which Governance & Compliance can impose applicable requirements, assess evidence, conduct compliance or certification activity, and manage findings without taking ownership of the Knowledge domain.

⸻

1\. Purpose

C8 menetapkan bagaimana Knowledge Integrity dalam PD-04 berhubungan dengan Governance & Compliance tanpa memindahkan ownership antara kedua domain.

C8 menjawab:

• apa yang dimaksud dengan integrity terhadap Knowledge;

• bagaimana integrity dipertahankan;

• bagaimana governance requirement berinteraksi dengan Knowledge;

• bagaimana evidence dan provenance dipertahankan;

• bagaimana compliance concern terhadap Knowledge ditangani;

• bagaimana governance assessment menerima evidence dari PD-04;

• bagaimana finding dan corrective action dikembalikan kepada Knowledge owner;

• bagaimana boundary PD-04 dan PD-03 dipertahankan.

⸻

2\. Source Foundation

Platform Organization menetapkan:

PD-03 → Governance & Compliance → Governance Authority

PD-04 → Knowledge & Intelligence → Knowledge Authority

PD-04 memiliki primary output:

Knowledge Assets

dan mengonsumsi:

Architecture

Governance

Document Relationship Matrix juga menempatkan Volume 4 sebagai otoritas terhadap domain Knowledge & Intelligence, dengan Governance sebagai salah satu relationship lintas platform.

5\. Fundamental Principle

Knowledge Integrity is owned by the Knowledge domain; governance assurance is exercised through the Governance & Compliance interface without transferring Knowledge ownership to the governance domain.

Model:

Knowledge Integrity

↓

PD-04 Ownership

↓

Governance Interface

↓

PD-03 Governance / Compliance

⸻

6\. Knowledge Integrity Definition

Untuk C8, Knowledge Integrity berarti:

kemampuan Knowledge ecosystem untuk mempertahankan identity, correctness, provenance, consistency, lifecycle state, authority, dan traceability yang diperlukan agar Knowledge dapat dipercaya dan digunakan sesuai scope-nya.

Knowledge Integrity bukan sekadar:

• data availability;

• database consistency;

• retrieval accuracy;

• security;

• compliance status.

⸻

7\. Integrity Dimensions

C8 menggunakan integrity dimensions berikut:

Identity

Provenance

Traceability

Consistency

Validity

Freshness

Authority

Lifecycle Integrity

Access Integrity

Context Integrity

Tidak semua dimension wajib memiliki implementation mechanism yang sama.

⸻

8\. Knowledge Identity Integrity

Setiap canonical Knowledge object harus memiliki identity yang dapat dibedakan.

Identity integrity mencegah:

• duplicate identity;

• ambiguous identity;

• accidental replacement;

• undocumented mutation.

⸻

9\. Provenance Integrity

Material Knowledge harus dapat ditelusuri kepada source atau origin yang relevan.

Canonical flow:

Source

↓

Knowledge Asset

↓

Transformation / Interpretation

↓

Knowledge

↓

Consumer

Provenance harus dipertahankan sepanjang lifecycle apabila material.

⸻

10\. Traceability Integrity

Knowledge harus dapat ditelusuri:

Source

↓

Knowledge

↓

Context / Relationship

↓

Consumer

↓

Outcome

Traceability memungkinkan:

• review;

• correction;

• audit;

• investigation;

• governance assessment.

⸻

11\. Consistency Integrity

Knowledge harus menjaga consistency terhadap:

• canonical terminology;

• Knowledge Model;

• semantic model;

• graph relationships;

• applicable state;

• authoritative source.

Consistency tidak berarti seluruh Knowledge harus identik.

⸻

12\. Validity Integrity

Knowledge harus memiliki basis untuk menentukan apakah knowledge tersebut masih valid dalam scope yang berlaku.

Validity dapat dipengaruhi oleh:

• source status;

• temporal validity;

• authority;

• supersession;

• contradiction;

• lifecycle state.

⸻

13\. Freshness Integrity

Knowledge yang berubah terhadap waktu harus dapat menunjukkan freshness apabila freshness material terhadap penggunaannya.

Model:

Fresh

↓

Aging

↓

Stale

↓

Expired / Superseded

Exact threshold tidak dibekukan oleh C8.

⸻

14\. Authority Integrity

Knowledge harus dapat dibedakan berdasarkan authority source.

Model:

Authoritative Source

↓

Knowledge

↓

Authority Metadata

Retrieval popularity tidak boleh menggantikan source authority.

⸻

15\. Lifecycle Integrity

Knowledge lifecycle harus mempertahankan state yang dapat dipahami.

Conceptual lifecycle:

Candidate

↓

Validated

↓

Active

↓

Reviewed

↓

Updated / Superseded

5\. lifecycle integrity dijaga;

6\. authority integrity dijaga;

7\. context integrity dijaga;

16\. certification interface jelas;

18\. security boundary tetap pada PD-08;

20\. quality boundary tetap pada PD-09;

21\. shared responsibility tidak menghasilkan dual ownership;

22\. historical integrity dipertahankan;

23\. silent repair terhadap material record dicegah;

24\. uncertainty dapat dinyatakan;

25\. implementation technology tetap terbuka;

26\. C9 dapat dibangun tanpa mengambil alih C8;

27\. C10 dapat menggunakan integrity outcomes sebagai success input.

73\. Canonical Knowledge Integrity Statement

Knowledge Integrity is the preservation of trustworthy identity, provenance, traceability, validity, consistency, authority, lifecycle, context, and applicable access conditions across the Knowledge & Intelligence domain.

⸻

74\. Canonical Governance Interface Statement

PD-04 owns Knowledge Integrity within the Knowledge & Intelligence domain. PD-03 owns Governance & Compliance and provides the applicable governance, compliance, control, certification, and assurance interface. Governance interaction does not transfer Knowledge ownership, implementation ownership, or domain authority from PD-04 to PD-03.

78\. Final Freeze Statement

C8 — Knowledge Integrity & Governance Interface is approved and frozen as a source-supported foundation with bounded reconstruction. The source establishes PD-04 as Knowledge Authority and establishes Governance as a cross-platform dependency/interface, while preserving domain ownership. C8 therefore defines the integrity boundary of Knowledge and the interface through which Governance & Compliance can impose applicable requirements, assess evidence, conduct compliance or certification activity, and manage findings without taking ownership of the Knowledge domain.

⸻

Final Status

C8 — Knowledge Integrity & Governance Interface: PASS — FROZEN WITH BOUNDED RECONSTRUCTION

Jika evidence baru bertentangan dengan bounded reconstruction C8, jangan menambal C8 secara lokal; lakukan reconciliation terhadap C8 dan predecessor yang terdampak sebelum freeze berikutnya.

1\. Purpose

C9 menetapkan architectural boundary untuk bagaimana Knowledge & Intelligence berkembang, diperbaiki, diperbarui, diperluas, dan ditingkatkan tanpa kehilangan:

• Knowledge integrity;

• Knowledge authority;

• semantic consistency;

• provenance;

• traceability;

• architectural alignment;

• governance compatibility;

• cross-platform boundary.

C9 menjawab:

Bagaimana Knowledge Architecture dapat berevolusi secara terkendali tanpa kehilangan integrity dan architectural coherence?

C9 tidak mendefinisikan ulang:

• Knowledge Constitution;

• Knowledge Model;

• Knowledge Asset Architecture;

• Repository Architecture;

• Semantic Architecture;

• Knowledge Graph Architecture;

• Context Knowledge;

• Governance Interface.

Concern tersebut tetap berada pada C1–C8.

⸻

2\. Source Position

Baseline platform organization menetapkan:

PD-04

Knowledge & Intelligence

↓

Knowledge Authority

PD-04 menghasilkan:

Knowledge Assets

dan memiliki relationship dengan:

Architecture

Governance

Divisi yang Akan Dibangun.txt

Baseline juga menetapkan prinsip:

Independent Evolution

Setiap Platform dapat berkembang tanpa mengubah Platform lain selama interface contract tetap dipenuhi.

Divisi yang Akan Dibangun.txt

Karena itu, evolution merupakan concern yang legitimate untuk PD-04, tetapi evolution harus tetap berada dalam domain Knowledge Authority.

⸻

dalam baseline PD-04 yang tersedia.

5\. Core Evolution Principle

Knowledge must evolve through controlled change that preserves applicable integrity, authority, traceability, architectural coherence, and cross-platform contracts.

Dengan demikian:

Evolution

≠

Uncontrolled Change

dan:

Improvement

≠

Arbitrary Redesign

⸻

6\. Knowledge Evolution Definition

Knowledge Evolution adalah perubahan terencana atau terkendali terhadap Knowledge Architecture atau Knowledge Assets untuk mempertahankan atau meningkatkan fitness terhadap kebutuhan AIOS.

Evolution dapat mencakup:

• refinement;

• correction;

• expansion;

• consolidation;

• restructuring;

• semantic improvement;

• lifecycle transition;

• capability improvement;

• maturity improvement.

Exact implementation mechanism tidak dibekukan oleh C9.

⸻

7\. Knowledge Improvement Definition

Knowledge Improvement adalah aktivitas yang bertujuan meningkatkan quality, usefulness, reliability, maintainability, accessibility, atau maturity dari Knowledge capability.

Model:

Current State

↓

Evidence

↓

Improvement Need

↓

Improvement Action

↓

Validation

↓

Improved State

⸻

8\. Evolution vs Improvement

Keduanya berhubungan tetapi tidak identik.

Evolution

↓

Perubahan keadaan / struktur / capability

Improvement

↓

Peningkatan terhadap keadaan yang ada

Sebuah improvement dapat menyebabkan evolution.

Tetapi tidak setiap evolution merupakan improvement.

Contoh:

Retirement

merupakan evolution, tetapi tidak selalu merupakan improvement.

⸻

9\. Evolution Drivers

Knowledge evolution dapat dipicu oleh:

New Knowledge Requirement

Knowledge Integrity Finding

Quality Finding

Governance Requirement

Architecture Change

Runtime Requirement

AI Engineering Requirement

Consumer Requirement

Knowledge Gap

Obsolescence

Semantic Conflict

Capability Maturity

Performance Evidence

Security Requirement

Tidak semua driver memiliki authority yang sama.

⸻

10\. Driver Authority

Driver hanya menjadi input.

Driver tidak otomatis menjadi decision authority.

Model:

Evolution Driver

↓

Analysis

↓

Impact Assessment

↓

Applicable Authority

↓

Evolution Decision

11\. Knowledge Gap

Knowledge gap terjadi ketika Knowledge capability yang tersedia tidak cukup untuk memenuhi kebutuhan yang legitimate.

Knowledge gap dapat berupa:

• missing knowledge;

• incomplete knowledge;

• insufficient context;

• insufficient relationship;

• stale knowledge;

• insufficient semantic coverage;

• insufficient provenance;

• insufficient consumer support.

Knowledge gap harus dibuktikan sebelum menjadi material evolution work.

⸻

12\. Obsolescence

Knowledge dapat menjadi obsolete karena:

• source berubah;

• authority berubah;

• architecture berubah;

• context berubah;

• lifecycle state berubah;

• knowledge superseded;

• consumer requirement berubah.

Obsolescence tidak boleh diatasi dengan silent deletion.

⸻

Trigger

↓

Detection

↓

Qualification

↓

Impact Assessment

↓

Decision

Trigger belum berarti change authorization.

72\. Success Criteria

C9 dianggap berhasil apabila:

0\. Knowledge evolution memiliki boundary yang jelas;

0\. improvement dibedakan dari arbitrary change;

0\. evolution drivers dapat diidentifikasi;

0\. evolution memiliki classification;

0\. materiality dapat menentukan review depth;

0\. impact assessment tersedia;

0\. Knowledge integrity C8 tetap preserved;

0\. provenance tetap preserved;

0\. semantic integrity tetap preserved;

0\. context integrity tetap preserved;

0\. graph integrity tetap preserved;

0\. repository integrity tetap preserved;

0\. consumer compatibility diperhatikan;

0\. architecture impact dapat dieskalasikan;

0\. governance impact dapat dieskalasikan;

0\. quality impact dapat dieskalasikan;

0\. security impact dapat dieskalasikan;

0\. authority boundary tidak berubah;

0\. cross-platform ownership tidak berubah;

0\. interface contracts dipertahankan;

0\. improvement membutuhkan evidence;

0\. maturity improvement membutuhkan evidence;

0\. autonomous evolution tidak berarti unrestricted modification;

0\. historical traceability dipertahankan;

0\. implementation technology tetap terbuka;

0\. C10 dapat menilai outcome evolution dan improvement.

73\. Canonical Evolution Statement

Knowledge Evolution is the controlled transformation of the PD-04 Knowledge Architecture and its Knowledge Assets to preserve relevance, integrity, coherence, capability, and fitness while maintaining applicable authority, traceability, governance, architecture, quality, security, and cross-platform boundaries.

⸻

74\. Canonical Improvement Statement

Knowledge Improvement is an evidence-based effort to increase the effectiveness, reliability, usefulness, maintainability, or maturity of Knowledge capability without weakening established integrity and architectural boundaries.

78\. Final Freeze Statement

C9 — Knowledge Evolution & Improvement Architecture is approved and frozen as a source-supported foundation with bounded reconstruction. The source establishes PD-04 as Knowledge Authority, establishes Knowledge Assets as its primary output, establishes formal Architecture and Governance relationships, and establishes independent Platform evolution subject to preserved interface contracts. C9 therefore defines the controlled evolution and improvement boundary for Knowledge without creating new authority, changing Platform ownership, or replacing the constitutional and integrity boundaries established by C1–C8.

⸻

Jika evidence tersebut menghasilkan identity atau boundary yang lebih authoritative, lakukan reconciliation terhadap C9 dan predecessor terkait sebelum construction, bukan menimpa bounded reconstruction secara diam-diam.

1\. Purpose

C9 menetapkan architectural boundary untuk bagaimana Knowledge & Intelligence berkembang, diperbaiki, diperbarui, diperluas, dan ditingkatkan tanpa kehilangan:

• Knowledge integrity;

• Knowledge authority;

• semantic consistency;

• provenance;

• traceability;

• architectural alignment;

• governance compatibility;

• cross-platform boundary.

C9 menjawab:

Bagaimana Knowledge Architecture dapat berevolusi secara terkendali tanpa kehilangan integrity dan architectural coherence?

C9 tidak mendefinisikan ulang:

• Knowledge Constitution;

• Knowledge Model;

• Knowledge Asset Architecture;

• Repository Architecture;

• Semantic Architecture;

• Knowledge Graph Architecture;

• Context Knowledge;

• Governance Interface.

Concern tersebut tetap berada pada C1–C8.

⸻

PD-04

Knowledge & Intelligence

Knowledge Authority

PD-04 menghasilkan:

Knowledge Assets

dan memiliki relationship dengan:

Architecture

Governance

Divisi yang Akan Dibangun.txt

Baseline juga menetapkan prinsip:

Independent Evolution

Setiap Platform dapat berkembang tanpa mengubah Platform lain selama interface contract tetap dipenuhi.

Divisi yang Akan Dibangun.txt

Karena itu, evolution merupakan concern yang legitimate untuk PD-04, tetapi evolution harus tetap berada dalam domain Knowledge Authority.

↓

C5

Knowledge Semantic Architecture

↓

C6

Knowledge Graph Architecture

↓

C7

Context Knowledge Architecture

↓

C8

Knowledge Integrity & Governance Interface

↓

↓

C9 adalah change and improvement layer, bukan replacement layer.

⸻

5\. Core Evolution Principle

Knowledge must evolve through controlled change that preserves applicable integrity, authority, traceability, architectural coherence, and cross-platform contracts.

Dengan demikian:

Evolution

≠

Uncontrolled Change

dan:

Improvement

≠

Arbitrary Redesign

⸻

6\. Knowledge Evolution Definition

Knowledge Evolution adalah perubahan terencana atau terkendali terhadap Knowledge Architecture atau Knowledge Assets untuk mempertahankan atau meningkatkan fitness terhadap kebutuhan AIOS.

Evolution dapat mencakup:

• refinement;

• correction;

• expansion;

• consolidation;

• restructuring;

• semantic improvement;

• lifecycle transition;

• capability improvement;

• maturity improvement.

Exact implementation mechanism tidak dibekukan oleh C9.

⸻

7\. Knowledge Improvement Definition

Knowledge Improvement adalah aktivitas yang bertujuan meningkatkan quality, usefulness, reliability, maintainability, accessibility, atau maturity dari Knowledge capability.

Model:

Current State

↓

Evidence

↓

Improvement Need

↓

Improvement Action

↓

Validation

↓

Improved State

⸻

8\. Evolution vs Improvement

Keduanya berhubungan tetapi tidak identik.

Evolution

↓

Perubahan keadaan / struktur / capability

Improvement

↓

Peningkatan terhadap keadaan yang ada

Sebuah improvement dapat menyebabkan evolution.

Tetapi tidak setiap evolution merupakan improvement.

Contoh:

Retirement

merupakan evolution, tetapi tidak selalu merupakan improvement.

⸻

9\. Evolution Drivers

Knowledge evolution dapat dipicu oleh:

New Knowledge Requirement

Knowledge Integrity Finding

Quality Finding

Governance Requirement

Architecture Change

Runtime Requirement

AI Engineering Requirement

Consumer Requirement

Knowledge Gap

Obsolescence

Semantic Conflict

Capability Maturity

Performance Evidence

Security Requirement

Tidak semua driver memiliki authority yang sama.

⸻

10\. Driver Authority

Driver hanya menjadi input.

Driver tidak otomatis menjadi decision authority.

Model:

Evolution Driver

↓

Analysis

↓

Impact Assessment

↓

Applicable Authority

↓

Evolution Decision

⸻

11\. Knowledge Gap

Knowledge gap terjadi ketika Knowledge capability yang tersedia tidak cukup untuk memenuhi kebutuhan yang legitimate.

Knowledge gap dapat berupa:

• missing knowledge;

• incomplete knowledge;

• insufficient context;

• insufficient relationship;

• stale knowledge;

• insufficient semantic coverage;

• insufficient provenance;

• insufficient consumer support.

Knowledge gap harus dibuktikan sebelum menjadi material evolution work.

⸻

12\. Obsolescence

Knowledge dapat menjadi obsolete karena:

• source berubah;

• authority berubah;

• architecture berubah;

• context berubah;

• lifecycle state berubah;

• knowledge superseded;

• consumer requirement berubah.

Obsolescence tidak boleh diatasi dengan silent deletion.

⸻

13\. Evolution Trigger

Canonical trigger model:

Trigger

↓

Detection

↓

Qualification

↓

Impact Assessment

↓

Decision

Trigger belum berarti change authorization.

⸻

14\. Evolution Classification

Secara conceptual, Knowledge evolution dapat diklasifikasikan sebagai:

Class Meaning

E0 Metadata / non-material refinement

E1 Knowledge correction

E2 Knowledge expansion

E3 Structural refinement

E4 Cross-interface change

E5 Material architectural evolution

Classification digunakan untuk menentukan review depth.

Exact thresholds tetap OPEN.

⸻

15\. E0 — Non-Material Refinement

Contoh:

• metadata correction;

• formatting;

• non-material labeling;

• documentation clarification.

E0 tidak boleh digunakan untuk menyembunyikan perubahan material.

⸻

16\. E1 — Knowledge Correction

Digunakan apabila Knowledge terbukti:

• incorrect;

• outdated;

• contradictory;

• incomplete;

• improperly attributed.

Correction harus mempertahankan applicable traceability.

⸻

17\. E2 — Knowledge Expansion

Knowledge expansion menambahkan:

• new Knowledge Asset;

• new context;

• new semantic relationship;

• new domain coverage;

• new Knowledge source.

Expansion harus divalidasi terhadap existing Knowledge Architecture.

⸻

18\. E3 — Structural Refinement

Structural refinement dapat memengaruhi:

• Knowledge organization;

• Knowledge model;

• semantic arrangement;

• graph relationship;

• repository organization.

Jika perubahan memengaruhi canonical architecture, escalation diperlukan.

⸻

19\. E4 — Cross-Interface Change

E4 memengaruhi interface dengan Platform lain.

Contoh:

PD-04

Knowledge

↓

PD-05 Runtime

atau:

PD-04

Knowledge

↓

PD-06 AI Engineering

atau:

PD-04

Knowledge

↓

PD-03 Governance

Cross-platform contract harus tetap dipenuhi.

⸻

20\. E5 — Material Architectural Evolution

E5 mencakup perubahan yang dapat memengaruhi:

• Knowledge Architecture Constitution;

• domain boundary;

• canonical Knowledge Model;

• major semantic architecture;

• major graph architecture;

• major repository architecture;

• cross-platform contract;

• authority boundary.

E5 tidak boleh dilakukan sebagai operational change biasa.

⸻

30\. Evolution Evidence

Material evolution harus memiliki evidence yang proporsional.

31\. Improvement Evidence

32\. No Unsupported Improvement Claim

Do not claim improvement solely because a change was implemented.

Implementation success:

Change Completed

tidak sama dengan:

Improvement Proven

Improvement membutuhkan evidence outcome yang relevan.

• interface boundaries.

72\. Success Criteria

C9 dianggap berhasil apabila:

1\. Knowledge evolution memiliki boundary yang jelas;

2\. improvement dibedakan dari arbitrary change;

3\. evolution drivers dapat diidentifikasi;

4\. evolution memiliki classification;

5\. materiality dapat menentukan review depth;

6\. impact assessment tersedia;

7\. Knowledge integrity C8 tetap preserved;

8\. provenance tetap preserved;

9\. semantic integrity tetap preserved;

10\. context integrity tetap preserved;

11\. graph integrity tetap preserved;

12\. repository integrity tetap preserved;

13\. consumer compatibility diperhatikan;

14\. architecture impact dapat dieskalasikan;

15\. governance impact dapat dieskalasikan;

16\. quality impact dapat dieskalasikan;

17\. security impact dapat dieskalasikan;

18\. authority boundary tidak berubah;

19\. cross-platform ownership tidak berubah;

20\. interface contracts dipertahankan;

21\. improvement membutuhkan evidence;

22\. maturity improvement membutuhkan evidence;

23\. autonomous evolution tidak berarti unrestricted modification;

24\. historical traceability dipertahankan;

25\. implementation technology tetap terbuka;

26\. C10 dapat menilai outcome evolution dan improvement.

⸻

73\. Canonical Evolution Statement

Knowledge Evolution is the controlled transformation of the PD-04 Knowledge Architecture and its Knowledge Assets to preserve relevance, integrity, coherence, capability, and fitness while maintaining applicable authority, traceability, governance, architecture, quality, security, and cross-platform boundaries.

⸻

74\. Canonical Improvement Statement

Knowledge Improvement is an evidence-based effort to increase the effectiveness, reliability, usefulness, maintainability, or maturity of Knowledge capability without weakening established integrity and architectural boundaries.

78\. Final Freeze Statement

C9 — Knowledge Evolution & Improvement Architecture is approved and frozen as a source-supported foundation with bounded reconstruction. The source establishes PD-04 as Knowledge Authority, establishes Knowledge Assets as its primary output, establishes formal Architecture and Governance relationships, and establishes independent Platform evolution subject to preserved interface contracts. C9 therefore defines the controlled evolution and improvement boundary for Knowledge without creating new authority, changing Platform ownership, or replacing the constitutional and integrity boundaries established by C1–C8.

⸻

1\. Purpose

C10 menetapkan kondisi yang digunakan untuk menentukan apakah Knowledge Architecture PD-04 telah mencapai keadaan yang dapat diterima sebagai successful.

C10 menjawab:

Apakah Knowledge Architecture mampu menghasilkan, menjaga, menggunakan, dan mengembangkan Knowledge secara reliable dalam boundary yang ditetapkan PD-04?

C10 tidak mengimplementasikan Knowledge.

C10 juga tidak menyatakan bahwa PD-04 secara aktual telah mencapai seluruh kondisi tersebut.

C10 menetapkan:

• success dimensions;

• success gates;

• evidence requirements;

• success status;

• failure conditions;

• improvement relationship;

• reassessment conditions;

• authority boundary.

⸻

5\. Success Constitution

Knowledge Architecture succeeds when Knowledge can be reliably represented, governed, preserved, contextualized, evolved, and consumed for legitimate AIOS needs while maintaining applicable authority, integrity, traceability, semantic coherence, and cross-platform boundaries.

Success bukan sekadar:

Knowledge exists

atau:

Repository exists

atau:

Knowledge Graph exists

Keberhasilan harus mencerminkan fitness of the integrated Knowledge Architecture.

⸻

6\. Success Scope

C10 menilai:

Knowledge Architecture

│

├── Constitution

├── Model

├── Assets

├── Repository

├── Semantics

├── Graph

├── Context

├── Integrity

└── Evolution

C10 tidak menilai secara langsung:

• Runtime Architecture;

• AI Engineering Architecture;

• Infrastructure Architecture;

• Security Architecture;

• Developer Experience;

• enterprise strategy;

• overall AIOS success.

⸻

⸻

8\. Dimension 1 — Knowledge Integrity

Knowledge Architecture harus mampu mempertahankan integrity Knowledge yang dikelolanya.

Integrity mencakup:

• identity;

• provenance;

• lifecycle state;

• traceability;

• consistency;

• applicable authority.

C8 merupakan upstream integrity authority.

C10 hanya menilai apakah integrity condition tersebut tercapai.

⸻

9\. Integrity Success Condition

Knowledge Integrity dianggap memenuhi success condition apabila:

Knowledge

↓

Identity

↓

Provenance

↓

Lifecycle

↓

Traceability

↓

Integrity Validation

dapat ditunjukkan secara evidence-based untuk Knowledge yang material.

⸻

10\. Integrity Failure

Critical integrity failure dapat memblokir overall success.

Contoh:

• provenance material hilang;

• Knowledge identity tidak dapat ditentukan;

• material contradiction tidak terdeteksi;

• lifecycle state tidak dapat dipercaya;

• traceability material hilang.

⸻

11\. Dimension 2 — Semantic Coherence

Knowledge harus memiliki semantic coherence yang cukup untuk digunakan secara konsisten.

Semantic coherence mencakup:

• terminology;

• concept identity;

• relationships;

• meaning;

• context;

• semantic compatibility.

C5 dan C6 menyediakan architecture foundation.

C10 menilai outcome-nya.

⸻

12\. Semantic Success Condition

Semantic Architecture dianggap successful apabila:

Concept

↓

Meaning

↓

Relationship

↓

Context

↓

Interpretation

dapat dipertahankan tanpa material ambiguity yang tidak diketahui.

⸻

13\. Semantic Failure

Potential failure:

• unresolved semantic conflict;

• duplicate concept identity;

• incompatible meaning;

• broken relationship;

• context-dependent meaning yang tidak ditandai.

Semantic failure yang material harus memblokir success claim sampai resolved atau formally accepted.

⸻

14\. Dimension 3 — Knowledge Utility

Knowledge harus dapat memberikan utility kepada legitimate consumers.

Baseline menetapkan PD-04 menghasilkan:

Knowledge Assets

dan memiliki relationship dengan:

PD-05 Runtime

PD-06 AI Engineering

dalam dependency architecture.

Divisi yang Akan Dibangun.txt

Utility bukan berarti semua consumer selalu mendapatkan semua Knowledge.

Utility berarti Knowledge dapat digunakan sesuai intended purpose dan access boundary.

⸻

15\. Utility Success Condition

Knowledge Utility dapat dinilai melalui:

• relevance;

• accessibility;

• contextual adequacy;

• consumer fitness;

• decision usefulness;

• retrieval/use effectiveness.

Exact numerical threshold tetap OPEN sampai authoritative source menetapkannya.

⸻

16\. Utility vs Availability

Knowledge Available

≠

Knowledge Useful

Repository availability tidak cukup untuk membuktikan Knowledge utility.

⸻

17\. Utility vs Quantity

More Knowledge

≠

Better Knowledge

Volume Knowledge bukan success metric dengan sendirinya.

⸻

18\. Dimension 4 — Architectural Coherence

Knowledge Architecture harus tetap coherent terhadap AIOS architecture.

PD-04 adalah domain owner Knowledge.

Architecture Office tetap memiliki architecture authority.

Relationship:

PD-02

Architecture Authority

↓

Architectural Constraints

↓

PD-04

Knowledge Authority

↓

Knowledge Architecture

Knowledge Architecture tidak boleh mengambil alih Architecture Authority.

⸻

19\. Architectural Coherence Success

Success condition:

• Knowledge boundary jelas;

• interfaces jelas;

• ownership jelas;

• dependencies dapat ditelusuri;

• architecture relationship tidak contradictory;

• cross-platform contracts tetap valid.

⸻

20\. Dimension 5 — Controlled Evolution

C9 menetapkan evolution and improvement architecture.

C10 menilai apakah evolution:

• controlled;

• evidence-based;

• traceable;

• authorized;

• reversible where applicable;

• validated.

Evolution

↓

Evidence

↓

Decision

↓

Change

↓

Validation

↓

Outcome

21\. Evolution Success

Evolution system successful apabila material changes tidak menghasilkan uncontrolled degradation terhadap:

• integrity;

• semantics;

• utility;

• architecture;

• authority.

⸻

22\. Integrated Success Model

Kelima dimensions harus dibaca sebagai integrated system:

Knowledge Architecture Success

\=

Integrity

AND

Semantic Coherence

AND

Knowledge Utility

AND

Architectural Coherence

AND

Controlled Evolution

AND merupakan architectural gate.

Success tidak ditentukan melalui simple average.

⸻

23\. No Average Success Rule

Contoh:

Integrity = PASS

Semantic = PASS

Utility = PASS

Architecture = PASS

Evolution = FAIL

Tidak boleh menghasilkan:

SUCCESSFUL

jika evolution failure bersifat critical.

⸻

24\. Critical Dimension

Tidak semua finding memiliki materiality yang sama.

Namun:

Material failure pada integrity, authority, atau architectural boundary tidak boleh disembunyikan oleh strength pada dimension lain.

⸻

25\. Success Gate

Canonical success gate:

Gate K-01

Knowledge Integrity

↓

Gate K-02

Semantic Coherence

↓

Gate K-04

⸻

26\. Gate K-01 — Integrity

PASS apabila:

• identity dapat ditentukan;

• provenance tersedia sesuai materiality;

• lifecycle state valid;

• material integrity issues tertangani.

FAIL apabila critical integrity condition tidak terpenuhi.

⸻

27\. Gate K-02 — Semantic Coherence

PASS apabila:

• material concepts memiliki coherent meaning;

• semantic relationships dapat dijelaskan;

• material ambiguity diketahui dan dikendalikan.

⸻

28\. Gate K-03 — Knowledge Utility

PASS apabila:

• legitimate consumers dapat menggunakan Knowledge;

• intended purpose dapat dipenuhi;

• Knowledge tidak hanya tersedia tetapi usable.

⸻

29\. Gate K-04 — Architectural Coherence

PASS apabila:

• domain boundary preserved;

• architecture interfaces valid;

• ownership preserved;

• applicable architecture constraints dipenuhi.

⸻

30\. Gate K-05 — Controlled Evolution

PASS apabila:

• material change memiliki evidence;

• authority teridentifikasi;

• change dapat ditelusuri;

• outcome dapat divalidasi;

• regression dapat dideteksi.

⸻

31\. Evidence Model

Success claim harus dapat ditelusuri:

Success Criterion

↓

Evidence

↓

Assessment

↓

Conclusion

Jika evidence tidak cukup:

Insufficient Evidence

↓

Do Not Claim Success

⸻

32\. Evidence Classes

Evidence dapat berasal dari:

• Knowledge records;

• provenance records;

• semantic validation;

• graph validation;

• repository validation;

• consumer evidence;

• architecture review;

• governance review;

• evolution records;

• improvement outcomes.

Exact evidence schema tetap implementation concern sampai source canonical menetapkannya.

⸻

33\. Evidence Sufficiency

Evidence sufficiency harus mempertimbangkan:

• materiality;

• reliability;

• completeness;

• traceability;

• recency;

• independence where required.

Evidence quantity bukan satu-satunya criterion.

⸻

34\. Success Assessment Status

C10 menggunakan status:

SUCCESSFUL

CONDITIONALLY SUCCESSFUL

NOT YET SUCCESSFUL

INSUFFICIENT EVIDENCE

⸻

35\. SUCCESSFUL

SUCCESSFUL berarti seluruh mandatory success conditions terpenuhi dan tidak terdapat unresolved critical failure.

⸻

36\. CONDITIONALLY SUCCESSFUL

CONDITIONALLY SUCCESSFUL dapat digunakan apabila:

• minor gap masih ada;

• gap diketahui;

• gap memiliki owner;

• remediation memiliki path;

• tidak ada critical integrity or authority failure.

Conditional success tidak boleh digunakan untuk menyembunyikan critical failure.

⸻

37\. NOT YET SUCCESSFUL

Digunakan apabila:

• mandatory condition belum terpenuhi;

• material improvement masih diperlukan;

• architecture belum memenuhi acceptance condition.

⸻

38\. INSUFFICIENT EVIDENCE

Digunakan apabila:

Evidence

↓

Insufficient

↓

Conclusion Cannot Be Trusted

C10 tidak boleh mengubah missing evidence menjadi assumed success.

⸻

39\. Success vs Implementation

Implementation Complete

≠

Architecture Successful

Implementasi menunjukkan change telah dilakukan.

Success menunjukkan outcome memenuhi architecture condition.

⸻

40\. Success vs Knowledge Quantity

Knowledge Quantity

≠

Knowledge Success

Success harus ditentukan berdasarkan fitness, integrity, semantic coherence, architecture coherence, dan controlled evolution.

⸻

41\. Success vs Consumer Satisfaction

Consumer feedback merupakan evidence.

Namun:

Consumer Satisfaction

≠

Automatic Success

Consumer outcome harus dibaca bersama integrity, architecture, dan governance constraints.

⸻

42\. Success vs Maturity

Maturity merupakan supporting dimension.

Maturity

↓

Capability Condition

tetapi:

High Maturity

≠

Automatic Success

PD-04 tidak boleh dianggap successful hanya karena maturity meningkat.

62\. Knowledge Architecture Success Statement

PD-04 Knowledge & Intelligence is successful when its Knowledge Architecture reliably preserves Knowledge integrity, maintains semantic coherence, provides legitimate Knowledge utility, remains architecturally coherent within AIOS, and evolves through controlled, evidence-based change without violating authority, governance, security, quality, or cross-platform boundaries.

69\. Success Criteria

C10 dianggap berhasil sebagai architecture definition apabila:

0\. Knowledge Architecture success memiliki definition yang eksplisit;

0\. success dimensions dapat dinilai secara terpisah;

0\. Knowledge Integrity menjadi mandatory condition;

0\. Semantic Coherence dapat dinilai;

0\. Knowledge Utility dapat dinilai;

0\. Architectural Coherence dapat dinilai;

0\. Controlled Evolution dapat dinilai;

0\. success menggunakan evidence;

0\. insufficient evidence dapat dinyatakan;

0\. critical failure dapat memblokir success;

0\. success tidak ditentukan melalui simple average;

0\. success status memiliki state yang eksplisit;

0\. conditional success tidak menyembunyikan critical failure;

0\. success gap dapat diidentifikasi;

0\. success gap dapat mengarah ke C9 improvement;

0\. improvement dapat menghasilkan reassessment;

0\. material change dapat memicu regression assessment;

0\. PD-04 tetap Knowledge Authority;

0\. PD-02 tetap Architecture Authority;

0\. PD-03 tetap Governance Authority;

0\. cross-platform ownership tetap preserved;

0\. C10 tidak menciptakan implementation authority baru;

0\. C10 tidak menyatakan overall AIOS success;

0\. C10 tetap compatible dengan future successor architecture;

0\. success conclusion dapat ditelusuri dari criterion → evidence → assessment → conclusion.

71\. Final Freeze Statement

C10 — Knowledge Architecture Success is approved and frozen as a source-supported foundation with bounded reconstruction. PD-04 is established as the Knowledge Authority responsible for Knowledge, Context, and Intelligence Assets, with Knowledge Model, Knowledge Repository, Semantic Layer, and Context Knowledge identified as core responsibilities. C10 therefore defines the terminal success assessment boundary for Part C without transferring Architecture, Governance, Security, Quality, Runtime, or AI Engineering authority to PD-04.

1\. Purpose

C10 menetapkan kondisi yang digunakan untuk menentukan apakah Knowledge Architecture PD-04 telah mencapai keadaan yang dapat diterima sebagai successful.

C10 menjawab:

Apakah Knowledge Architecture mampu menghasilkan, menjaga, menggunakan, dan mengembangkan Knowledge secara reliable dalam boundary yang ditetapkan PD-04?

C10 tidak mengimplementasikan Knowledge.

C10 juga tidak menyatakan bahwa PD-04 secara aktual telah mencapai seluruh kondisi tersebut.

C10 menetapkan:

• success dimensions;

• success gates;

• evidence requirements;

• success status;

• failure conditions;

• improvement relationship;

• reassessment conditions;

• authority boundary.

⸻

5\. Success Constitution

Knowledge Architecture succeeds when Knowledge can be reliably represented, governed, preserved, contextualized, evolved, and consumed for legitimate AIOS needs while maintaining applicable authority, integrity, traceability, semantic coherence, and cross-platform boundaries.

Success bukan sekadar:

Knowledge exists

atau:

Repository exists

atau:

Knowledge Graph exists

Keberhasilan harus mencerminkan fitness of the integrated Knowledge Architecture.

⸻

6\. Success Scope

C10 menilai:

Knowledge Architecture

│

├── Constitution

├── Model

├── Assets

├── Repository

├── Semantics

├── Graph

├── Context

├── Integrity

└── Evolution

C10 tidak menilai secara langsung:

• Runtime Architecture;

• AI Engineering Architecture;

• Infrastructure Architecture;

• Security Architecture;

• Developer Experience;

• enterprise strategy;

• overall AIOS success.

⸻

7\. Success Dimensions

Knowledge Architecture success terdiri dari dimensi berikut:

2\. Semantic Coherence

3\. Knowledge Utility

4\. Architectural Coherence

⸻

8\. Dimension 1 — Knowledge Integrity

Knowledge Architecture harus mampu mempertahankan integrity Knowledge yang dikelolanya.

Integrity mencakup:

• identity;

• provenance;

• lifecycle state;

• traceability;

• consistency;

• applicable authority.

C8 merupakan upstream integrity authority.

C10 hanya menilai apakah integrity condition tersebut tercapai.

⸻

9\. Integrity Success Condition

Knowledge Integrity dianggap memenuhi success condition apabila:

Knowledge

↓

Identity

↓

Provenance

↓

Lifecycle

↓

Traceability

↓

Integrity Validation

dapat ditunjukkan secara evidence-based untuk Knowledge yang material.

⸻

10\. Integrity Failure

Critical integrity failure dapat memblokir overall success.

Contoh:

• provenance material hilang;

• Knowledge identity tidak dapat ditentukan;

• material contradiction tidak terdeteksi;

• lifecycle state tidak dapat dipercaya;

• traceability material hilang.

⸻

11\. Dimension 2 — Semantic Coherence

Knowledge harus memiliki semantic coherence yang cukup untuk digunakan secara konsisten.

Semantic coherence mencakup:

• terminology;

• concept identity;

• relationships;

• meaning;

• context;

• semantic compatibility.

C5 dan C6 menyediakan architecture foundation.

C10 menilai outcome-nya.

⸻

12\. Semantic Success Condition

Semantic Architecture dianggap successful apabila:

Concept

↓

Meaning

↓

Relationship

↓

Context

↓

Interpretation

dapat dipertahankan tanpa material ambiguity yang tidak diketahui.

⸻

13\. Semantic Failure

Potential failure:

• unresolved semantic conflict;

• duplicate concept identity;

• incompatible meaning;

• broken relationship;

• context-dependent meaning yang tidak ditandai.

Semantic failure yang material harus memblokir success claim sampai resolved atau formally accepted.

⸻

14\. Dimension 3 — Knowledge Utility

Knowledge harus dapat memberikan utility kepada legitimate consumers.

Baseline menetapkan PD-04 menghasilkan:

Knowledge Assets

dan memiliki relationship dengan:

PD-05 Runtime

PD-06 AI Engineering

dalam dependency architecture.

Divisi yang Akan Dibangun.txt

Utility bukan berarti semua consumer selalu mendapatkan semua Knowledge.

Utility berarti Knowledge dapat digunakan sesuai intended purpose dan access boundary.

⸻

15\. Utility Success Condition

Knowledge Utility dapat dinilai melalui:

• relevance;

• accessibility;

• contextual adequacy;

• consumer fitness;

• decision usefulness;

• retrieval/use effectiveness.

Exact numerical threshold tetap OPEN sampai authoritative source menetapkannya.

⸻

16\. Utility vs Availability

Knowledge Available

≠

Knowledge Useful

Repository availability tidak cukup untuk membuktikan Knowledge utility.

⸻

17\. Utility vs Quantity

More Knowledge

≠

Better Knowledge

Volume Knowledge bukan success metric dengan sendirinya.

⸻

18\. Dimension 4 — Architectural Coherence

Knowledge Architecture harus tetap coherent terhadap AIOS architecture.

PD-04 adalah domain owner Knowledge.

Architecture Office tetap memiliki architecture authority.

Relationship:

PD-02

Architecture Authority

↓

Architectural Constraints

↓

PD-04

Knowledge Authority

↓

Knowledge Architecture

Knowledge Architecture tidak boleh mengambil alih Architecture Authority.

⸻

19\. Architectural Coherence Success

Success condition:

• Knowledge boundary jelas;

• interfaces jelas;

• ownership jelas;

• dependencies dapat ditelusuri;

• architecture relationship tidak contradictory;

• cross-platform contracts tetap valid.

⸻

20\. Dimension 5 — Controlled Evolution

C9 menetapkan evolution and improvement architecture.

C10 menilai apakah evolution:

• controlled;

• evidence-based;

• traceable;

• authorized;

• reversible where applicable;

• validated.

Model:

Evolution

↓

Evidence

↓

Decision

↓

Change

↓

Validation

↓

Outcome

⸻

21\. Evolution Success

Evolution system successful apabila material changes tidak menghasilkan uncontrolled degradation terhadap:

• integrity;

• semantics;

• utility;

• architecture;

• authority.

⸻

22\. Integrated Success Model

Kelima dimensions harus dibaca sebagai integrated system:

Knowledge Architecture Success

\=

Integrity

AND

Semantic Coherence

AND

Knowledge Utility

AND

Architectural Coherence

AND

Controlled Evolution

AND merupakan architectural gate.

Success tidak ditentukan melalui simple average.

⸻

23\. No Average Success Rule

Contoh:

Integrity = PASS

Semantic = PASS

Utility = PASS

Architecture = PASS

Evolution = FAIL

Tidak boleh menghasilkan:

SUCCESSFUL

jika evolution failure bersifat critical.

⸻

24\. Critical Dimension

Tidak semua finding memiliki materiality yang sama.

Namun:

Material failure pada integrity, authority, atau architectural boundary tidak boleh disembunyikan oleh strength pada dimension lain.

⸻

25\. Success Gate

Canonical success gate:

Gate K-01

Knowledge Integrity

↓

Gate K-02

Gate K-04

Architectural Coherence

⸻

26\. Gate K-01 — Integrity

PASS apabila:

• identity dapat ditentukan;

• provenance tersedia sesuai materiality;

• lifecycle state valid;

• material integrity issues tertangani.

FAIL apabila critical integrity condition tidak terpenuhi.

⸻

27\. Gate K-02 — Semantic Coherence

PASS apabila:

• material concepts memiliki coherent meaning;

• semantic relationships dapat dijelaskan;

• material ambiguity diketahui dan dikendalikan.

⸻

28\. Gate K-03 — Knowledge Utility

PASS apabila:

• legitimate consumers dapat menggunakan Knowledge;

• intended purpose dapat dipenuhi;

• Knowledge tidak hanya tersedia tetapi usable.

⸻

29\. Gate K-04 — Architectural Coherence

PASS apabila:

• domain boundary preserved;

• architecture interfaces valid;

• ownership preserved;

• applicable architecture constraints dipenuhi.

⸻

30\. Gate K-05 — Controlled Evolution

PASS apabila:

• material change memiliki evidence;

• authority teridentifikasi;

• change dapat ditelusuri;

• outcome dapat divalidasi;

• regression dapat dideteksi.

⸻

31\. Evidence Model

Success claim harus dapat ditelusuri:

Success Criterion

↓

Evidence

↓

Assessment

↓

Conclusion

Jika evidence tidak cukup:

Insufficient Evidence

↓

Do Not Claim Success

⸻

32\. Evidence Classes

Evidence dapat berasal dari:

• Knowledge records;

• provenance records;

• semantic validation;

• graph validation;

• repository validation;

• consumer evidence;

• architecture review;

• governance review;

• evolution records;

• improvement outcomes.

Exact evidence schema tetap implementation concern sampai source canonical menetapkannya.

⸻

33\. Evidence Sufficiency

Evidence sufficiency harus mempertimbangkan:

• materiality;

• reliability;

• completeness;

• traceability;

• recency;

• independence where required.

Evidence quantity bukan satu-satunya criterion.

⸻

34\. Success Assessment Status

C10 menggunakan status:

SUCCESSFUL

CONDITIONALLY SUCCESSFUL

NOT YET SUCCESSFUL

INSUFFICIENT EVIDENCE

⸻

35\. SUCCESSFUL

SUCCESSFUL berarti seluruh mandatory success conditions terpenuhi dan tidak terdapat unresolved critical failure.

⸻

36\. CONDITIONALLY SUCCESSFUL

CONDITIONALLY SUCCESSFUL dapat digunakan apabila:

• minor gap masih ada;

• gap diketahui;

• gap memiliki owner;

• remediation memiliki path;

• tidak ada critical integrity or authority failure.

Conditional success tidak boleh digunakan untuk menyembunyikan critical failure.

⸻

37\. NOT YET SUCCESSFUL

Digunakan apabila:

• mandatory condition belum terpenuhi;

• material improvement masih diperlukan;

• architecture belum memenuhi acceptance condition.

⸻

38\. INSUFFICIENT EVIDENCE

Digunakan apabila:

Evidence

↓

Insufficient

↓

Conclusion Cannot Be Trusted

C10 tidak boleh mengubah missing evidence menjadi assumed success.

⸻

39\. Success vs Implementation

Implementation Complete

≠

Architecture Successful

Implementasi menunjukkan change telah dilakukan.

Success menunjukkan outcome memenuhi architecture condition.

⸻

40\. Success vs Knowledge Quantity

Knowledge Quantity

≠

Knowledge Success

Success harus ditentukan berdasarkan fitness, integrity, semantic coherence, architecture coherence, dan controlled evolution.

⸻

41\. Success vs Consumer Satisfaction

Consumer feedback merupakan evidence.

Namun:

Consumer Satisfaction

≠

Automatic Success

Consumer outcome harus dibaca bersama integrity, architecture, dan governance constraints.

⸻

42\. Success vs Maturity

Maturity merupakan supporting dimension.

Maturity

↓

Capability Condition

tetapi:

High Maturity

≠

Automatic Success

PD-04 tidak boleh dianggap successful hanya karena maturity meningkat.

⸻

46\. Success Reassessment Triggers

Reassessment dapat dipicu oleh:

• material Knowledge change;

• material semantic change;

• repository architecture change;

• graph architecture change;

• context architecture change;

• cross-platform contract change;

• major governance change;

• major architecture change;

• material integrity incident.

Exact cadence belum dibekukan.

56\. Success Review Quality Controls

Review harus memeriksa:

K-QR-01 — Evidence Sufficiency

Apakah evidence cukup?

K-QR-02 — Criterion Validity

Apakah criterion memang relevan?

K-QR-03 — Integrity

Apakah Knowledge integrity terjaga?

K-QR-04 — Semantic Coherence

Apakah semantic meaning valid?

K-QR-05 — Context Completeness

Apakah context material diperhitungkan?

K-QR-06 — Authority

Apakah conclusion berada pada authority yang benar?

K-QR-07 — Boundary

Apakah review tetap berada dalam PD-04?

K-QR-08 — Traceability

Apakah conclusion dapat ditelusuri?

Critical failure pada:

• Knowledge Integrity;

• Authority;

• Architecture Boundary;

• material traceability;

dapat memblokir overall success.

62\. Knowledge Architecture Success Statement

PD-04 Knowledge & Intelligence is successful when its Knowledge Architecture reliably preserves Knowledge integrity, maintains semantic coherence, provides legitimate Knowledge utility, remains architecturally coherent within AIOS, and evolves through controlled, evidence-based change without violating authority, governance, security, quality, or cross-platform boundaries.

69\. Success Criteria

C10 dianggap berhasil sebagai architecture definition apabila:

1\. Knowledge Architecture success memiliki definition yang eksplisit;

2\. success dimensions dapat dinilai secara terpisah;

3\. Knowledge Integrity menjadi mandatory condition;

4\. Semantic Coherence dapat dinilai;

5\. Knowledge Utility dapat dinilai;

6\. Architectural Coherence dapat dinilai;

7\. Controlled Evolution dapat dinilai;

8\. success menggunakan evidence;

9\. insufficient evidence dapat dinyatakan;

10\. critical failure dapat memblokir success;

11\. success tidak ditentukan melalui simple average;

12\. success status memiliki state yang eksplisit;

13\. conditional success tidak menyembunyikan critical failure;

14\. success gap dapat diidentifikasi;

15\. success gap dapat mengarah ke C9 improvement;

16\. improvement dapat menghasilkan reassessment;

17\. material change dapat memicu regression assessment;

18\. PD-04 tetap Knowledge Authority;

19\. PD-02 tetap Architecture Authority;

20\. PD-03 tetap Governance Authority;

21\. cross-platform ownership tetap preserved;

22\. C10 tidak menciptakan implementation authority baru;

23\. C10 tidak menyatakan overall AIOS success;

24\. C10 tetap compatible dengan future successor architecture;

25\. success conclusion dapat ditelusuri dari criterion → evidence → assessment → conclusion.

⸻

71\. Final Freeze Statement

C10 — Knowledge Architecture Success is approved and frozen as a source-supported foundation with bounded reconstruction. PD-04 is established as the Knowledge Authority responsible for Knowledge, Context, and Intelligence Assets, with Knowledge Model, Knowledge Repository, Semantic Layer, and Context Knowledge identified as core responsibilities. C10 therefore defines the terminal success assessment boundary for Part C without transferring Architecture, Governance, Security, Quality, Runtime, or AI Engineering authority to PD-04.

⸻

Construction boundary: Part C secara struktural lengkap.

Source qualification: C10 belum terbukti sebagai identity literal dalam baseline; karena itu statusnya tetap bounded reconstruction sampai Claude Code menemukan evidence canonical yang lebih authoritative.

Arahan Claude Code: lakukan source reconciliation C10 sebelum implementasi substantif. Jika tidak ditemukan source tambahan, pertahankan C10 sebagai bounded reconstruction dan jangan mengubah threshold, scoring, atau assessment mechanism menjadi fakta canonical.
